We offer two GreenRFM configurations: (i) a performant model that establishes a new state-of-the-art using a single 24GB GPU within 24 hours, and (ii) a lightweight model that matches existing benchmarks with 6GB VRAM in 4 hours. We conduct extensive experiments using over 200,000 images from four institutions and of two modalities. GreenRFMs achieve superior performances on chest and abdominal CT datasets, regardless of public or private benchmark, surpassing a range of baseline models. In addition, the results on internal musculoskeletal MRI images show that the same supervision principles transfer between different modalities. Our performance and efficiency challenge the ``scale is all you need'' dogma and democratize the equitable development of state-of-the-art RFMs for clinicians even on a laptop.

</details>


### [114] [Match4Annotate: Propagating Sparse Video Annotations via Implicit Neural Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06471)
*Zhuorui Zhang,Roger Pallarès-López,Praneeth Namburi,Brian W. Anthony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Match4Annotate：基于SIREN隐式神经表示的轻量级视频标注传播框架，支持点与掩码标注的帧内/帧间传播，在临床超声数据集上实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业领域（如医学影像）的视频标注成本高昂且耗时，现有标注传播方法存在局限性：视频跟踪器需要逐视频初始化且无法跨视频泛化；传统对应性方法在低纹理场景表现不佳；密集特征匹配和单次分割方法缺乏时空平滑性且不支持点与掩码的统一处理

Method: 提出Match4Annotate框架：1）在测试时拟合基于SIREN的隐式神经表示到DINOv3特征，生成连续高分辨率时空特征场；2）学习帧对之间的平滑隐式变形场以指导对应性匹配；3）支持点与掩码标注的帧内和帧间传播

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的临床超声数据集上评估：1）帧间传播性能达到SOTA，优于特征匹配和单次分割基线；2）帧内传播与专用跟踪器保持竞争力；3）证明了轻量级测试时优化特征匹配流程在可扩展标注工作流中的潜力

Conclusion: Match4Annotate为专业领域的视频标注提供了一种高效、可访问的解决方案，通过隐式神经表示和变形场学习实现了高质量的标注传播，平衡了跨视频泛化能力和时空平滑性要求

Abstract: Acquiring per-frame video annotations remains a primary bottleneck for deploying computer vision in specialized domains such as medical imaging, where expert labeling is slow and costly. Label propagation offers a natural solution, yet existing approaches face fundamental limitations. Video trackers and segmentation models can propagate labels within a single sequence but require per-video initialization and cannot generalize across videos. Classic correspondence pipelines operate on detector-chosen keypoints and struggle in low-texture scenes, while dense feature matching and one-shot segmentation methods enable cross-video propagation but lack spatiotemporal smoothness and unified support for both point and mask annotations. We present Match4Annotate, a lightweight framework for both intra-video and inter-video propagation of point and mask annotations. Our method fits a SIREN-based implicit neural representation to DINOv3 features at test time, producing a continuous, high-resolution spatiotemporal feature field, and learns a smooth implicit deformation field between frame pairs to guide correspondence matching. We evaluate on three challenging clinical ultrasound datasets. Match4Annotate achieves state-of-the-art inter-video propagation, outperforming feature matching and one-shot segmentation baselines, while remaining competitive with specialized trackers for intra-video propagation. Our results show that lightweight, test-time-optimized feature matching pipelines have the potential to offer an efficient and accessible solution for scalable annotation workflows.

</details>


### [115] [Self-Supervised Flow Matching for Scalable Multi-Modal Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06507)
*Hila Chefer,Patrick Esser,Dominik Lorenz,Dustin Podell,Vikash Raja,Vinh Tong,Antonio Torralba,Robin Rombach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Self-Flow：一种自监督流匹配范式，通过双时间步调度机制在生成框架中集成表示学习，无需外部模型即可学习强语义表示并提升生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖外部模型存在三个问题：需要单独训练、目标不一致、缩放行为不可预测。这种依赖源于生成模型的训练目标（去噪任务）缺乏学习语义表示的激励

Method: 提出Self-Flow自监督流匹配范式，核心机制是双时间步调度：在不同token上应用异质噪声水平，创建信息不对称，迫使模型从被破坏的输入中推断缺失信息，从而同时学习强表示和生成能力

Result: 方法跨模态通用，支持多模态训练，遵循预期的缩放定律，在图像、视频和音频生成方面达到优越性能

Conclusion: Self-Flow通过将表示学习整合到生成框架中，消除了对外部模型的依赖，解决了现有方法的局限性，实现了语义表示学习和生成能力的协同提升

Abstract: Strong semantic representations improve the convergence and generation quality of diffusion and flow models. Existing approaches largely rely on external models, which require separate training, operate on misaligned objectives, and exhibit unexpected scaling behavior. We argue that this dependence arises from the model's training objective, which poses a denoising task with little incentive to learn semantic representations. We introduce Self-Flow: a self-supervised flow matching paradigm that integrates representation learning within the generative framework. Our key mechanism, Dual-Timestep Scheduling, applies heterogeneous noise levels across tokens, creating an information asymmetry that forces the model to infer missing information from corrupted inputs. This drives learning strong representations alongside generative capabilities without external supervision. Our method generalizes across modalities and enables multi-modal training while following expected scaling laws, achieving superior image, video, and audio generation.

</details>


### [116] [NEGATE: Constrained Semantic Guidance for Linguistic Negation in Text-to-Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06533)
*Taewon Kang,Ming C. Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种在扩散模型中形式化处理语言否定的方法，将否定视为扩散动态中语义引导的结构化可行性约束，无需启发式方法或重新训练模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定是基本的语言运算符，但在基于扩散的生成系统中建模不足，需要一种形式化方法来处理语言否定，以解决现有方法的局限性。

Method: 将无分类器引导重新解释为定义语义更新方向，并通过将更新投影到从语言结构导出的凸约束集上来强制执行否定，提供统一的框架处理多种否定现象。

Result: 方法实现了稳健的否定遵从性，同时保持视觉保真度和结构连贯性，建立了扩散模型中语言否定的第一个统一表述，超越了表示级评估。

Conclusion: 提出了扩散模型中语言否定的形式化处理方法，通过结构化可行性约束实现训练免费、与预训练扩散主干兼容的统一框架，并引入了专门的基准套件来推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Negation is a fundamental linguistic operator, yet it remains inadequately modeled in diffusion-based generative systems. In this work, we present a formal treatment of linguistic negation in diffusion-based generative models by modeling it as a structured feasibility constraint on semantic guidance within diffusion dynamics. Rather than introducing heuristics or retraining model parameters, we reinterpret classifier-free guidance as defining a semantic update direction and enforce negation by projecting the update onto a convex constraint set derived from linguistic structure. This novel formulation provides a unified framework for handling diverse negation phenomena, including object absence, graded non-inversion semantics, multi-negation composition, and scope-sensitive disambiguation. Our approach is training-free, compatible with pretrained diffusion backbones, and naturally extends from image generation to temporally evolving video trajectories. In addition, we introduce a structured negation-centric benchmark suite that isolates distinct linguistic failure modes in generative systems, to further research in this area. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves robust negation compliance while preserving visual fidelity and structural coherence, establishing the first unified formulation of linguistic negation in diffusion-based generative models beyond representation-level evaluation.

</details>


### [117] [Modeling and Measuring Redundancy in Multisource Multimodal Data for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06544)
*Yuhan Zhou,Mehri Sattari,Haihua Chen,Kewei Sha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析了自动驾驶数据集中冗余作为数据质量因素的影响，通过去除多源摄像头和多模态图像-LiDAR数据中的冗余标签来提升目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶研究主要关注算法设计而忽视了数据质量分析，特别是冗余这一基础但未被充分探索的数据质量问题。数据质量因环境和传感器限制在不同来源和模态间存在差异，影响自动驾驶的实时决策。

Method: 使用nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集，对多源摄像头数据和多模态图像-LiDAR数据中的冗余进行建模和测量。通过选择性去除共享视野摄像头中的冗余图像目标标签，评估对YOLOv8目标检测任务的影响。

Result: 在nuScenes中，去除冗余标签后三个代表性重叠区域的mAP50分别从0.66提升到0.70、0.64到0.67、0.53到0.55。在AV2中，移除了4.1-8.6%的标签，mAP50保持在0.64基线附近。多模态分析显示图像和LiDAR数据之间存在显著冗余。

Conclusion: 冗余是可测量且可操作的数据质量因素，直接影响自动驾驶性能。该研究强调了冗余作为数据质量因素在自动驾驶感知中的作用，并推动以数据为中心的视角来评估和改进自动驾驶数据集。

Abstract: Next-generation autonomous vehicles (AVs) rely on large volumes of multisource and multimodal ($M^2$) data to support real-time decision-making. In practice, data quality (DQ) varies across sources and modalities due to environmental conditions and sensor limitations, yet AV research has largely prioritized algorithm design over DQ analysis. This work focuses on redundancy as a fundamental but underexplored DQ issue in AV datasets. Using the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 (AV2) datasets, we model and measure redundancy in multisource camera data and multimodal image-LiDAR data, and evaluate how removing redundant labels affects the YOLOv8 object detection task. Experimental results show that selectively removing redundant multisource image object labels from cameras with shared fields of view improves detection. In nuScenes, mAP${50}$ gains from $0.66$ to $0.70$, $0.64$ to $0.67$, and from $0.53$ to $0.55$, on three representative overlap regions, while detection on other overlapping camera pairs remains at the baseline even under stronger pruning. In AV2, $4.1$-$8.6\%$ of labels are removed, and mAP${50}$ stays near the $0.64$ baseline. Multimodal analysis also reveals substantial redundancy between image and LiDAR data. These findings demonstrate that redundancy is a measurable and actionable DQ factor with direct implications for AV performance. This work highlights the role of redundancy as a data quality factor in AV perception and motivates a data-centric perspective for evaluating and improving AV datasets. Code, data, and implementation details are publicly available at: https://github.com/yhZHOU515/RedundancyAD

</details>


### [118] [EgoReasoner: Learning Egocentric 4D Reasoning via Task-Adaptive Structured Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06561)
*Fangrui Zhu,Yunfeng Xi,Jianmo Ni,Mu Cai,Boqing Gong,Long Zhao,Chen Qu,Ian Miao,Yi Li,Cheng Zhong,Huaizu Jiang,Shwetak Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoReasoner：针对自我中心视频4D推理任务的两阶段框架，通过任务自适应思维模板和任务感知奖励函数，在少量样本训练下显著超越更大模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频理解因环境的动态4D特性而复杂，现有任务无关方法（通用思维链和统一强化学习）无法适应不同认知结构任务的需求，导致性能不足

Method: 提出EgoReasoner两阶段框架：第一阶段使用任务自适应思维模板指导结构化思维链生成，通过监督微调使模型适应不同任务类型；第二阶段设计任务感知奖励函数验证实体定位、时间对齐和逻辑一致性，通过GRPO进行强化微调

Result: 仅用16K样本训练的3B参数模型在HD-EPIC基准上达到37.5%平均准确率，显著超越Qwen2.5-VL-7B（25.7%）超过10个百分点

Conclusion: 通过将推理框架和奖励信号与任务认知结构对齐，EgoReasoner有效解决了自我中心4D推理任务的多样性需求，在少量数据下实现了优于更大模型的性能

Abstract: Egocentric video understanding is inherently complex due to the dynamic 4D nature of the environment, where camera motion and object displacements necessitate a continuous re-evaluation of spatial relations. In this work, we target a suite of under-explored egocentric 4D reasoning tasks, including fixture interaction counting, viewpoint-relative fixture location, object movement itinerary tracking, and stationary object localization, that require fundamentally different cognitive operations: spatial anchoring, temporal tracking, and duration reasoning. We observe that these structural differences make task-agnostic approaches insufficient: generic Chain-of-Thought methods lack task-appropriate reasoning primitives, and uniform reinforcement learning actively destabilizes performance on spatial tasks. To address this, we propose EgoReasoner, a two-stage framework that aligns both the reasoning scaffold and the reward signal to each task's cognitive structure. In the first stage, Task-Adaptive Thinking Templates guide the synthesis of structured CoT traces that teach the model to reason adaptively across task types via supervised fine-tuning. In the second stage, task-aware reward functions verify entity grounding, temporal alignment, and task-adaptive logical consistency, selectively strengthening each reasoning pathway via reinforcement fine-tuning with GRPO. Our 3B-parameter model, trained on only 16K samples, achieves 37.5% average accuracy on the challenging HD-EPIC benchmark, surpassing Qwen2.5-VL-7B (25.7%) by over 10 points.

</details>


### [119] [Penguin-VL: Exploring the Efficiency Limits of VLM with LLM-based Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06569)
*Boqiang Zhang,Lei Ke,Ruihan Yang,Qi Gao,Tianyuan Qu,Rossell Chen,Dong Yu,Leoweiliang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Penguin-VL提出了一种轻量级视觉语言模型，通过使用基于文本LLM初始化的视觉编码器替代传统的对比预训练视觉编码器，在保持紧凑架构的同时实现了与大型VLMs相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM发展主要依赖模型规模扩展，这限制了在移动和边缘设备上的部署。同时，主流VLMs依赖对比预训练（如CLIP/SigLIP）初始化的视觉编码器存在目标不匹配问题：对比学习优化的判别性特征会抑制细粒度视觉线索，而这些线索对于密集描述和复杂推理至关重要。

Method: 提出Penguin-VL，其视觉编码器从纯文本LLM初始化，而非传统的对比预训练视觉编码器。这种方法解决了目标不匹配问题，能够保留更精细的空间和时间线索。模型采用轻量级架构（2B和8B参数规模），在保持计算效率的同时提升视觉表示质量。

Result: Penguin-VL在各种图像和视频基准测试中表现优异：在数学推理上与领先VLMs（如Qwen3-VL）相当，在文档理解、视觉知识和多视角视频理解等任务中超越它们。消融实验表明Penguin-Encoder始终优于对比预训练的编码器，保留了密集感知和复杂推理所需的关键细粒度线索。

Conclusion: 研究表明改进视觉表示而非模型缩放是性能提升的主要驱动力。Penguin-Encoder作为传统对比预训练编码器的强大替代方案，能够在资源受限环境中实现高性能，为计算高效的VLMs提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Language Model (VLM) development has largely relied on scaling model size, which hinders deployment on compute-constrained mobile and edge devices such as smartphones and robots. In this work, we explore the performance limits of compact (e.g., 2B and 8B) VLMs. We challenge the prevailing practice that state-of-the-art VLMs must rely on vision encoders initialized via massive contrastive pretraining (e.g., CLIP/SigLIP). We identify an objective mismatch: contrastive learning, optimized for discrimination, enforces coarse and category-level invariances that suppress fine-grained visual cues needed for dense captioning and complex VLM reasoning. To address this issue, we present Penguin-VL, whose vision encoder is initialized from a text-only LLM. Our experiments reveal that Penguin-Encoder serves as a superior alternative to traditional contrastive pretraining, unlocking a higher degree of visual fidelity and data efficiency for multimodal understanding. Across various image and video benchmarks, Penguin-VL achieves performance comparable to leading VLMs (e.g., Qwen3-VL) in mathematical reasoning and surpasses them in tasks such as document understanding, visual knowledge, and multi-perspective video understanding. Notably, these gains are achieved with a lightweight architecture, demonstrating that improved visual representation rather than model scaling is the primary driver of performance. Our ablations show that Penguin-Encoder consistently outperforms contrastive-pretrained encoders, preserving fine-grained spatial and temporal cues that are critical for dense perception and complex reasoning. This makes it a strong drop-in alternative for compute-efficient VLMs and enables high performance in resource-constrained settings. Code: https://github.com/tencent-ailab/Penguin-VL

</details>


### [120] [Omni-Diffusion: Unified Multimodal Understanding and Generation with Masked Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06577)
*Lijiang Li,Zuwei Long,Yunhang Shen,Heting Gao,Haoyu Cao,Xing Sun,Caifeng Shan,Ran He,Chaoyou Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Omni-Diffusion是首个完全基于掩码离散扩散模型的任意模态到任意模态的多模态语言模型，统一了文本、语音和图像的理解与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型主要采用自回归架构，在架构设计上存在探索有效且高效替代方案的空间。离散扩散模型在视觉理解和图像生成等领域显示出作为多模态系统骨干的巨大潜力。

Method: 采用统一的掩码离散扩散模型直接捕捉离散多模态标记的联合分布，支持双模态任务以及涉及多个模态的复杂场景。

Result: 在多样化基准测试中，该方法优于或与处理两个及以上模态的现有多模态系统表现相当。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在驱动下一代多模态基础模型方面具有显著潜力，Omni-Diffusion展示了基于离散扩散模型构建统一多模态系统的可行性。

Abstract: While recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made impressive strides, they predominantly employ a conventional autoregressive architecture as their backbone, leaving significant room to explore effective and efficient alternatives in architectural design. Concurrently, recent studies have successfully applied discrete diffusion models to various domains, such as visual understanding and image generation, revealing their considerable potential as a promising backbone for multimodal systems. Drawing inspiration from these pioneering research, we introduce Omni-Diffusion, the first any-to-any multimodal language model built entirely on mask-based discrete diffusion models, which unifies understanding and generation across text, speech, and images. Omni-Diffusion employs a unified mask-based discrete diffusion model to directly capture the joint distribution over discrete multimodal tokens. This approach supports not only bimodal tasks but also more complex scenarios involving multiple modalities. On a diverse set of benchmarks, our method outperforms or performs on par with existing multimodal systems that process two or more modalities, highlighting the significant promise of diffusion models in powering the next generation of multimodal foundation models. Project webpage: https://omni-diffusion.github.io.

</details>


### [121] [Multimodal Large Language Models as Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06578)
*Nikita Kisel,Illia Volkov,Klara Janouskova,Jiri Matas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究发现多模态大语言模型分类性能评估存在协议缺陷和标注质量问题，通过修正评估协议和标注噪声，显著缩小了MLLM与监督模型的性能差距，并展示了MLLM辅助标注的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于多模态大语言模型分类性能的研究存在矛盾结论，这些矛盾源于评估协议缺陷（如丢弃有效输出、弱干扰项导致结果虚高）和标注质量问题，需要系统分析并修正这些问题。

Method: 1) 识别并修正常见评估协议的关键问题；2) 量化常被忽视的设计选择（批量大小、图像顺序、文本编码器）的影响；3) 使用ReGT（ImageNet-1k的625类多标签重新标注）评估；4) 进行受控案例研究评估MLLM辅助标注效果。

Result: 1) 修正标注噪声可使MLLM性能提升高达+10.8%；2) 评估协议缺陷和标注噪声是MLLM表现不佳的主要原因而非模型缺陷；3) 较少依赖监督训练信号的模型对标注质量最敏感；4) 在困难案例中，标注者确认或整合MLLM预测的比例约50%。

Conclusion: MLLM在分类任务中的表现不佳很大程度上是评估协议缺陷和标注噪声的产物，而非模型本身缺陷。修正这些问题可显著提升性能，且MLLM具有辅助大规模数据集标注的潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLM) classification performance depends critically on evaluation protocol and ground truth quality. Studies comparing MLLMs with supervised and vision-language models report conflicting conclusions, and we show these conflicts stem from protocols that either inflate or underestimate performance. Across the most common evaluation protocols, we identify and fix key issues: model outputs that fall outside the provided class list and are discarded, inflated results from weak multiple-choice distractors, and an open-world setting that underperforms only due to poor output mapping. We additionally quantify the impact of commonly overlooked design choices - batch size, image ordering, and text encoder selection - showing they substantially affect accuracy. Evaluating on ReGT, our multilabel reannotation of 625 ImageNet-1k classes, reveals that MLLMs benefit most from corrected labels (up to +10.8%), substantially narrowing the perceived gap with supervised models. Much of the reported MLLMs underperformance on classification is thus an artifact of noisy ground truth and flawed evaluation protocol rather than genuine model deficiency. Models less reliant on supervised training signals prove most sensitive to annotation quality. Finally, we show that MLLMs can assist human annotators: in a controlled case study, annotators confirmed or integrated MLLMs predictions in approximately 50% of difficult cases, demonstrating their potential for large-scale dataset curation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [VDCook:DIY video data cook your MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05539)
*Chengwei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VDCook是一个自演进的视频数据操作系统，通过自然语言查询和参数配置，自动执行视频检索与可控合成，生成带有完整溯源和元数据的领域特定数据包，支持数据集动态演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态数据集构建成本高、难以更新，研究者需要更灵活、可扩展的视频数据构建平台来降低专业视频训练数据集的构建门槛。

Method: 用户通过自然语言查询和参数配置发起数据请求，系统自动进行查询优化，并行运行真实视频检索和可控合成模块，生成带有完整溯源、元数据和多维标注的数据包。

Result: 构建了可配置的视频数据构建平台，支持数据集的动态演进和领域扩展，提供多维元数据标注（场景分割、运动评分、OCR比率、自动字幕等），降低专业视频数据集构建门槛。

Conclusion: VDCook通过基础设施级解决方案将数据集转变为动态演进的开源生态系统，支持社区贡献和治理驱动的数据扩展范式，显著降低了专业视频训练数据集的构建门槛。

Abstract: We introduce VDCook: a self-evolving video data operating system, a configurable video data construction platform for researchers and vertical domain teams. Users initiate data requests via natural language queries and adjustable parameters (scale, retrieval-synthesis ratio, quality threshold). The system automatically performs query optimization, concurrently running real video retrieval and controlled synthesis modules. It ultimately generates in-domain data packages with complete provenance and metadata, along with reproducible Notebooks.
  Unlike traditional static, one-time-built datasets, VDCook enables continuous updates and domain expansion through its automated data ingestion mechanism based on MCP (Model Context Protocol)\cite{mcp2024anthropic}, transforming datasets into dynamically evolving open ecosystems. The system also provides multi-dimensional metadata annotation (scene segmentation, motion scoring, OCR ratio, automatic captioning, etc.), laying the foundation for flexible subsequent data `cooking' and indexing\cite{vlogger}.
  This platform aims to significantly lower the barrier to constructing specialized video training datasets through infrastructure-level solutions, while supporting community contributions and a governance-enabled data expansion paradigm. \textbf{Project demo:} https://screenapp.io/app/v/WP0SvffgsH

</details>


### [123] [IntSeqBERT: Learning Arithmetic Structure in OEIS via Modulo-Spectrum Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05556)
*Kazuhisa Nakasho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IntSeqBERT：用于OEIS整数序列建模的双流Transformer，通过连续对数尺度幅度嵌入和模数嵌入处理大范围数值，在幅度准确性和模数预测上显著优于标准token化Transformer基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OEIS中的整数序列数值范围极大（从个位数到天文级阶乘和指数），标准token化模型无法处理词汇表外数值，也无法利用周期性算术结构，因此需要专门设计来处理这些挑战

Method: 提出IntSeqBERT双流Transformer编码器：1）连续对数尺度幅度嵌入；2）100个模数（2-101）的正弦/余弦模数嵌入；3）通过FiLM融合双流；4）三个预测头联合训练（幅度回归、符号分类、100个模数预测）；5）基于概率中国剩余定理的求解器将预测转换为具体整数

Result: 大型模型（9150万参数）在测试集上达到95.85%幅度准确性和50.38%平均模数准确性，分别比标准token化Transformer基线高8.9和4.5个百分点；消融实验显示模数流贡献了15.2个百分点的MMA增益和6.2个百分点的幅度准确性提升；下一项预测Top-1准确率19.09% vs 2.59%（7.4倍提升）；模数谱分析显示归一化信息增益与欧拉函数比φ(m)/m呈强负相关（r=-0.851）

Conclusion: IntSeqBERT通过双流架构有效处理大范围整数序列，模数嵌入能高效捕捉OEIS的算术结构，复合模数通过CRT聚合更有效地捕获算术结构，为整数序列建模提供了新方法

Abstract: Integer sequences in the OEIS span values from single-digit constants to astronomical factorials and exponentials, making prediction challenging for standard tokenised models that cannot handle out-of-vocabulary values or exploit periodic arithmetic structure. We present IntSeqBERT, a dual-stream Transformer encoder for masked integer-sequence modelling on OEIS. Each sequence element is encoded along two complementary axes: a continuous log-scale magnitude embedding and sin/cos modulo embeddings for 100 residues (moduli $2$--$101$), fused via FiLM. Three prediction heads (magnitude regression, sign classification, and modulo prediction for 100 moduli) are trained jointly on 274,705 OEIS sequences. At the Large scale (91.5M parameters), IntSeqBERT achieves 95.85% magnitude accuracy and 50.38% Mean Modulo Accuracy (MMA) on the test set, outperforming a standard tokenised Transformer baseline by $+8.9$ pt and $+4.5$ pt, respectively. An ablation removing the modulo stream confirms it accounts for $+15.2$ pt of the MMA gain and contributes an additional $+6.2$ pt to magnitude accuracy. A probabilistic Chinese Remainder Theorem (CRT)-based Solver converts the model's predictions into concrete integers, yielding a 7.4-fold improvement in next-term prediction over the tokenised-Transformer baseline (Top-1: 19.09% vs. 2.59%). Modulo spectrum analysis reveals a strong negative correlation between Normalised Information Gain (NIG) and Euler's totient ratio $\varphi(m)/m$ ($r = -0.851$, $p < 10^{-28}$), providing empirical evidence that composite moduli capture OEIS arithmetic structure more efficiently via CRT aggregation.

</details>


### [124] [A recipe for scalable attention-based MLIPs: unlocking long-range accuracy with all-to-all node attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06567)
*Eric Qu,Brandon M. Wood,Aditi S. Krishnapriyan,Zachary W. Ulissi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AllScAIP是一种基于注意力机制、能量守恒的机器学习原子间势能模型，通过全节点注意力机制处理长程相互作用，可扩展到1亿训练样本，在分子系统上达到最先进的能量/力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习原子间势能模型在处理大系统（如生物分子和电解质）时难以准确捕获长程相互作用，现有方法依赖显式的物理基项或组件，需要开发能有效处理长程相互作用且可扩展的模型。

Method: 提出AllScAIP模型，采用基于注意力的能量守恒架构，核心创新是数据驱动的全节点注意力组件，用于处理长程相互作用。模型可扩展到1亿训练样本，通过广泛的消融实验验证设计选择。

Result: 在分子系统上达到最先进的能量/力精度，在物理基评估（OMol25）上表现优异，在材料（OMat24）和催化剂（OC20）基准上具有竞争力。能够进行稳定、长时间尺度的分子动力学模拟，准确预测实验可观测值如密度和汽化热。

Conclusion: 在低数据/小模型情况下，归纳偏置能提高样本效率；但随着数据和模型规模扩大，这些优势减弱甚至逆转，而全节点注意力对捕获长程相互作用至关重要。AllScAIP为大规模原子系统模拟提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have advanced rapidly, with many top models relying on strong physics-based inductive biases. However, as models scale to larger systems like biomolecules and electrolytes, they struggle to accurately capture long-range (LR) interactions, leading current approaches to rely on explicit physics-based terms or components. In this work, we propose AllScAIP, a straightforward, attention-based, and energy-conserving MLIP model that scales to O(100 million) training samples. It addresses the long-range challenge using an all-to-all node attention component that is data-driven. Extensive ablations reveal that in low-data/small-model regimes, inductive biases improve sample efficiency. However, as data and model size scale, these benefits diminish or even reverse, while all-to-all attention remains critical for capturing LR interactions. Our model achieves state-of-the-art energy/force accuracy on molecular systems, as well as a number of physics-based evaluations (OMol25), while being competitive on materials (OMat24) and catalysts (OC20). Furthermore, it enables stable, long-timescale MD simulations that accurately recover experimental observables, including density and heat of vaporization predictions.

</details>


### [125] [Autocorrelation effects in a stochastic-process model for decision making via time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05559)
*Tomoki Yamagami,Mikio Hasegawa,Takatomo Mihana,Ryoichi Horisaki,Atsushi Uchida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究分析了基于时间序列的决策模型，发现自相关特性对多臂赌博机问题求解性能的影响取决于环境条件：在奖励丰富的环境中负自相关最优，在奖励贫乏的环境中正自相关最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前实验发现基于半导体激光器混沌动力学的决策系统中，决策精度强烈依赖于混沌波形的时间自相关特性，但缺乏能够解释这种自相关优势的最小数学模型。

Method: 采用基于时间序列的随机过程模型，结合拔河原理解决两臂赌博机问题，模型中阈值和二元马尔可夫信号共同演化，通过数值模拟分析自相关特性对决策性能的影响。

Result: 数值结果显示环境依赖的结构：在奖励丰富的环境中负自相关最优，在奖励贫乏的环境中正自相关最优。当获胜概率之和大于1时负自相关有利，小于1时正自相关有利，等于1时性能与自相关无关。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了时间序列自相关特性对决策性能的影响机制，为改进无线通信和机器人应用中强化学习方案的决策机制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Decision makers exploiting photonic chaotic dynamics obtained by semiconductor lasers provide an ultrafast approach to solving multi-armed bandit problems by using a temporal optical signal as the driving source for sequential decisions. In such systems, the sampling interval of the chaotic waveform shapes the temporal correlation of the resulting time series, and experiments have reported that decision accuracy depends strongly on this autocorrelation property. However, it remains unclear whether the benefit of autocorrelation can be explained by a minimal mathematical model. Here, we analyze a stochastic-process model of the time-series-based decision making using the tug-of-war principle for solving the two-armed bandit problem, where the threshold and a two-valued Markov signal evolve jointly. Numerical results reveal an environment-dependent structure: negative (positive) autocorrelation is optimal in reward-rich (reward-poor) environments. These findings show that negative autocorrelation of the time series is advantageous when the sum of the winning probabilities is more than $1$, whereas positive autocorrelation is useful when the sum of the winning probabilities is less than $1$. Moreover, the performance is independent of autocorrelation if the sum of the winning probabilities equals $1$, which is mathematically clarified. This study paves the way for improving the decision-making scheme for reinforcement learning applications in wireless communications and robotics.

</details>


### [126] [Towards Efficient and Stable Ocean State Forecasting: A Continuous-Time Koopman Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05560)
*Rares Grozavescu,Pengyu Zhang,Mark Girolami,Etienne Meunier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CT-KAE作为轻量级替代模型用于两层准地转系统的长期海洋状态预测，通过将非线性动力学投影到线性ODE控制的潜在空间，实现结构化、可解释的时间演化，并通过矩阵指数公式实现时间分辨率不变的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发高效稳定的混合物理-机器学习气候模型，解决长期海洋状态预测中传统数值求解器计算成本高、自回归Transformer基线在长期滚动中误差放大和能量漂移的问题。

Method: 使用连续时间Koopman自编码器（CT-KAE），将非线性动力学投影到由线性常微分方程控制的潜在空间，通过矩阵指数公式实现时间分辨率不变的预测，并与自回归Transformer基线进行对比。

Result: 在2083天的滚动预测中，CT-KAE表现出有界的误差增长和稳定的大尺度统计特性，而Transformer基线则显示逐渐的误差放大和能量漂移。虽然小尺度湍流结构部分耗散，但整体能量谱、涡度演化和自相关结构在长期范围内保持一致。推理速度比数值求解器快几个数量级。

Conclusion: 连续时间Koopman替代模型为高效稳定的混合物理-机器学习气候模型提供了有前景的基础架构，在长期海洋状态预测中展现出优越的稳定性和计算效率。

Abstract: We investigate the Continuous-Time Koopman Autoencoder (CT-KAE) as a lightweight surrogate model for long-horizon ocean state forecasting in a two-layer quasi-geostrophic (QG) system. By projecting nonlinear dynamics into a latent space governed by a linear ordinary differential equation, the model enforces structured and interpretable temporal evolution while enabling temporally resolution-invariant forecasting via a matrix exponential formulation. Across 2083-day rollouts, CT-KAE exhibits bounded error growth and stable large-scale statistics, in contrast to autoregressive Transformer baselines which exhibit gradual error amplification and energy drift over long rollouts. While fine-scale turbulent structures are partially dissipated, bulk energy spectra, enstrophy evolution, and autocorrelation structure remain consistent over long horizons. The model achieves orders-of-magnitude faster inference compared to the numerical solver, suggesting that continuous-time Koopman surrogates offer a promising backbone for efficient and stable hybrid physical-machine learning climate models.

</details>


### [127] [When AI Levels the Playing Field: Skill Homogenization, Asset Concentration, and Two Regimes of Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05565)
*Xupeng Chen,Shuchen Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生成式AI压缩任务内技能差异，但将经济价值转向集中的互补资产，形成个体表现均等化却可能加剧总体不平等的悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成式AI如何通过压缩个体技能差异同时将价值转向集中资产，创造个体平等与总体不平等之间的理论张力，为理解AI对劳动力市场不平等的影响提供机制框架。

Method: 构建包含内生教育、雇主筛选和异质性企业的任务基础模型，通过模拟矩方法匹配六个经验目标进行情景分析，使用BLS OEWS数据进行职业层面回归验证模型局限性。

Result: 模型识别出两种制度边界取决于AI技术结构（专有vs商品）和劳动力市场制度；校准参数显示总体不平等符号由特定矩和弹性决定，而AI技术结构独立跨越边界；职业层面数据无法检验任务层面预测。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于揭示机制而非给出确定结论，预测需要尚未大规模存在的职业内、任务内面板数据进行检验，为理解AI对不平等的影响提供了理论框架和实证方向。

Abstract: Generative AI compresses within-task skill differences while shifting economic value toward concentrated complementary assets, creating an apparent paradox: the technology that equalizes individual performance may widen aggregate inequality. We formalize this tension in a task-based model with endogenous education, employer screening, and heterogeneous firms. The model yields two regimes whose boundary depends on AI's technology structure (proprietary vs.\ commodity) and labor market institutions (rent-sharing elasticity, asset concentration). A scenario analysis via Method of Simulated Moments, matching six empirical targets, disciplines the model's quantitative magnitudes; a sensitivity decomposition reveals that the five non-$Δ$Gini moments identify mechanism rates but not the aggregate sign, which at the calibrated parameters is pinned by $m_6$ and $ξ$, while AI's technology structure ($η_1$ vs.\ $η_0$) independently crosses the boundary. The contribution is the mechanism -- not a verdict on the sign. Occupation-level regressions using BLS OEWS data (2019--2023) illustrate why such data cannot test the model's task-level predictions. The predictions are testable with within-occupation, within-task panel data that do not yet exist at scale.

</details>


### [128] [Why Depth Matters in Parallelizable Sequence Models: A Lie Algebraic View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05573)
*Gyuryang Heo,Timothy Ngotiaoco,Kazuki Irie,Samuel J. Gershman,Bernardo Sabatini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从李代数控制视角分析序列模型的表达能力边界，建立了模型深度与李代数扩展塔的对应关系，证明了误差随深度指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可扩展序列模型（如Transformer变体和结构化状态空间模型）通常在表达能力与序列级并行性之间进行权衡。需要从理论层面理解当模型在其表达能力范围之外操作时，误差的边界和缩放规律。

Method: 采用李代数控制视角，建立序列模型深度与李代数扩展塔的对应关系。理论分析恒定深度序列模型的李代数类别及其表达能力边界，解析推导近似误差边界。

Result: 理论分析表明误差随深度增加呈指数衰减，这与这些模型的强大实证性能一致。在符号词和连续值状态跟踪问题上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 从李代数控制视角为序列模型的表达能力提供了理论框架，建立了深度与误差缩放之间的定量关系，解释了深度模型性能优越的理论基础。

Abstract: Scalable sequence models, such as Transformer variants and structured state-space models, often trade expressivity power for sequence-level parallelism, which enables efficient training. Here we examine the bounds on error and how error scales when models operate outside of their expressivity regimes using a Lie-algebraic control perspective. Our theory formulates a correspondence between the depth of a sequence model and the tower of Lie algebra extensions. Echoing recent theoretical studies, we characterize the Lie-algebraic class of constant-depth sequence models and their corresponding expressivity bounds. Furthermore, we analytically derive an approximation error bound and show that error diminishes exponentially as the depth increases, consistent with the strong empirical performance of these models. We validate our theoretical predictions using experiments on symbolic word and continuous-valued state-tracking problems.

</details>


### [129] [A Novel Hybrid Heuristic-Reinforcement Learning Optimization Approach for a Class of Railcar Shunting Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05579)
*Ruonan Zhao,Joseph Geunes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合启发式-强化学习框架，用于解决铁路货场中具有双侧分类轨道访问的双机车列车编组问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路货场编组是货运铁路的核心规划任务，需要分解和重组车厢组以形成出站列车。传统单侧分类轨道遵循后进先出原则，而双侧轨道允许先进先出操作。解决具有双侧轨道访问和双机车的列车编组问题具有组合复杂性挑战。

Method: 将问题分解为两个子问题，每个子问题具有单侧分类轨道访问和一侧的机车。提出混合启发式-强化学习框架，将铁路特定启发式方法与Q-learning强化学习方法集成。该框架利用方法减少状态-动作空间并在强化学习过程中引导探索。

Result: 通过一系列数值实验证明，HHRL算法在单侧访问单机车问题和双侧访问双机车问题中均表现出高效性和高质量。

Conclusion: 提出的混合启发式-强化学习框架能够有效解决具有双侧分类轨道访问的铁路货场列车编组问题，通过分解策略和状态空间优化方法提高了算法性能。

Abstract: Railcar shunting is a core planning task in freight railyards, where yard planners need to disassemble and reassemble groups of railcars to form outbound trains. Classification tracks with access from one side only can be considered as stack structures, where railcars are added and removed from only one end, leading to a last-in-first-out (LIFO) retrieval order. In contrast, two-sided tracks function like queue structures, allowing railcars to be added from one end and removed from the opposite end, following a first-in-first-out (FIFO) order. We consider a problem requiring assembly of multiple outbound trains using two locomotives in a railyard with two-sided classification track access. To address this combinatorially challenging problem class, we decompose the problem into two subproblems, each with one-sided classification track access and a locomotive on each side. We present a novel Hybrid Heuristic-Reinforcement Learning (HHRL) framework that integrates railway-specific heuristic solution approaches with a reinforcement learning method, specifically Q-learning. The proposed framework leverages methods to decrease the state-action space and guide exploration during reinforcement learning. The results of a series of numerical experiments demonstrate the efficiency and quality of the HHRL algorithm in both one-sided access, single-locomotive problems and two-sided access, two-locomotive problems.

</details>


### [130] [On the Value of Tokeniser Pretraining in Physics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05598)
*Hadi Sotoudeh,Payel Mukhopadhyay,Ruben Ohana,Michael McCabe,Neil D. Lawrence,Shirley Ho,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究tokenizer预训练对物理仿真模型精度和效率的影响，发现在相同物理系统上预训练能显著提升性能，并提出支持运行时调整压缩比的时空压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高分辨率仿真产生海量多物理场数据，训练基础模型学习这些数据的动力学对于建模复杂多物理现象至关重要。然而，同时学习数据压缩表示和物理动力学两个任务会相互干扰，影响模型效果。

Method: 采用tokenizer预训练策略，先通过自编码目标预训练tokenizer，再训练动力学模型。引入灵活的时空压缩操作，扩展因果卷积以支持运行时可调整的压缩比，适应不同下游任务。

Result: 领域内预训练（相同物理系统）在10,500训练步后使VRMSE降低64%，相比从头训练显著提升。领域外预训练（其他物理系统）也能带来中等收益。预训练效果取决于领域对齐程度。

Conclusion: tokenizer预训练能显著提升物理基础模型的效率和精度，领域对齐是关键因素。提出的灵活时空压缩方法支持高效适应不同下游任务，为训练高效物理仿真器提供实用指导。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of tokeniser pretraining on the accuracy and efficiency of physics emulation. Modern high-resolution simulations produce vast volumes of data spanning diverse physical regimes and scales. Training foundation models to learn the dynamics underlying such data enables the modelling of complex multiphysics phenomena, especially in data-limited settings. The emerging class of physics foundation models typically aims to learn two tasks jointly: (i) extracting compact representations of high-resolution spatiotemporal data, and (ii) capturing governing physical dynamics. However, learning both tasks from scratch simultaneously can impede the effectiveness of either process. We demonstrate that pretraining the tokeniser with an autoencoding objective prior to training the dynamics model enhances computational efficiency for downstream tasks. Notably, the magnitude of this benefit depends on domain alignment: pretraining on the same physical system as the downstream task yields the largest improvements, while pretraining on other systems provides moderate gains. In-domain pretraining reduces VRMSE by 64% after 10,500 training steps compared to training from scratch. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic investigation of tokeniser pretraining for physics foundation models. We further introduce flexible spatiotemporal compression operations that extend causal convolutions to support runtime-adjustable compression ratios, enabling efficient adaptation to diverse downstream tasks. Our findings provide practical guidance for training efficient physics emulators and highlight the importance of strategic pretraining data selection.

</details>


### [131] [Identifying Adversary Characteristics from an Observed Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05625)
*Soyon Choi,Scott Alfeld,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个识别攻击者特征的防御框架，而非直接防御攻击本身，通过分析攻击行为推断最可能的攻击者身份


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在自动决策系统中易受数据操纵攻击，现有防御机制主要针对攻击本身或系统层面，缺乏对攻击者身份的分析，而攻击者信息对防御策略制定有重要价值

Method: 提出领域无关的框架，从观察到的攻击中识别攻击者特征；首先证明攻击者在无额外知识时不可识别，然后设计框架推断最可能的攻击者；框架支持外源性缓解（修改决策系统或限制攻击者能力）和针对性防御（如对抗正则化）

Result: 框架在多种学习器上进行了具体实例化，证明了其适用性；通过攻击者识别，防御者可以更有效地制定防御策略，提高对抗攻击的性能

Conclusion: 从攻击者角度而非攻击本身进行防御是可行的新方向，攻击者识别框架为防御策略提供了重要补充，有助于实现更全面的安全防护

Abstract: When used in automated decision-making systems, machine learning (ML) models are vulnerable to data-manipulation attacks. Some defense mechanisms (e.g., adversarial regularization) directly affect the ML models while others (e.g., anomaly detection) act within the broader system. In this paper we consider a different task for defending the adversary, focusing on the attacker, rather than the attack. We present and demonstrate a framework for identifying characteristics about the attacker from an observed attack.
  We prove that, without additional knowledge, the attacker is non-identifiable (multiple potential attackers would perform the same observed attack). To address this challenge, we propose a domain-agnostic framework to identify the most probable attacker. This framework aids the defender in two ways. First, knowledge about the attacker can be leveraged for exogenous mitigation (i.e., addressing the vulnerability by altering the decision-making system outside the learning algorithm and/or limiting the attacker's capability). Second, when implementing defense methods that directly affect the learning process (e.g., adversarial regularization), knowledge of the specific attacker improves performance. We present the details of our framework and illustrate its applicability through specific instantiations on a variety of learners.

</details>


### [132] [The Value of Graph-based Encoding in NBA Salary Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05671)
*Junhao Su,David Grimsman,Christopher Archibald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过构建包含场上和场外数据的知识图谱，将其嵌入向量空间，并将该向量纳入表格数据中，可以显著提升NBA球员薪资预测的准确性，特别是对老将和高薪球员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业运动员的市场价值评估具有挑战性，因为球员表现和位置每年都有很大变化。传统基于表格数据的监督学习方法对于年轻球员（合同主要基于选秀顺位）效果良好，但对于老将或高薪球员预测效果不佳。

Method: 构建包含场上和场外数据的知识图谱，使用多种图嵌入算法将图谱嵌入向量空间，然后将得到的向量表示纳入传统的表格数据中，结合监督学习进行薪资预测。

Result: 比较了多种图嵌入算法，证明将知识图谱嵌入向量纳入表格数据的方法对于NBA薪资预测至关重要，能够更好地理解影响薪资的各种因素。

Conclusion: 通过整合知识图谱嵌入到传统监督学习框架中，可以显著提升NBA球员薪资预测的准确性，特别是对于传统方法难以处理的老将和高薪球员群体。

Abstract: Market valuations for professional athletes is a difficult problem, given the amount of variability in performance and location from year to year. In the National Basketball Association (NBA), a straightforward way to address this problem is to build a tabular data set and use supervised machine learning to predict a player's salary based on the player's performance in the previous year. For younger players, whose contracts are mostly built on draft position, this approach works well, however it can fail for veterans or those whose salaries are on the high tail of the distribution. In this paper, we show that building a knowledge graph with on and off court data, embedding that graph in a vector space, and including that vector in the tabular data allows the supervised learning to better understand the landscape of factors that affect salary. We compare several graph embedding algorithms and show that such a process is vital to NBA salary prediction.

</details>


### [133] [Improved Scaling Laws via Weak-to-Strong Generalization in Random Feature Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05691)
*Diyuan Wu,Lehan Chen,Theodor Misiakiewicz,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了机器学习中弱到强泛化现象，发现在随机特征岭回归框架下，使用弱教师模型生成标签训练强学生模型可以显著改进测试误差的缩放规律，学生甚至可以达到极小极大最优速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中常见使用学习模型标注数据，再用这些数据训练更强模型的做法。弱到强泛化现象表明，用弱教师的不完美标签训练强学生，学生能超越教师性能。本文旨在量化这种改进潜力，特别是研究其对测试误差缩放规律的影响。

Method: 采用随机特征岭回归（RFRR）框架分析教师和学生模型。主要技术贡献是推导了学生使用教师生成标签训练时，其超出测试误差的确定性等价表达式。通过该表达式识别学生缩放规律优于教师的机制。

Result: 研究发现学生测试误差的缩放规律可以显著优于教师，这种改进在偏置主导和方差主导的设置中都能实现。最引人注目的是，学生可能达到极小极大最优速率，即使教师的测试误差不随样本量衰减。

Conclusion: 弱到强泛化过程能实质性地改进模型性能的缩放规律，学生模型可以超越教师的性能限制，甚至达到理论最优速率。这为使用弱监督数据训练强模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: It is increasingly common in machine learning to use learned models to label data and then employ such data to train more capable models. The phenomenon of weak-to-strong generalization exemplifies the advantage of this two-stage procedure: a strong student is trained on imperfect labels obtained from a weak teacher, and yet the strong student outperforms the weak teacher. In this paper, we show that the potential improvement is substantial, in the sense that it affects the scaling law followed by the test error. Specifically, we consider students and teachers trained via random feature ridge regression (RFRR). Our main technical contribution is to derive a deterministic equivalent for the excess test error of the student trained on labels obtained via the teacher. Via this deterministic equivalent, we then identify regimes in which the scaling law of the student improves upon that of the teacher, unveiling that the improvement can be achieved both in bias-dominated and variance-dominated settings. Strikingly, the student may attain the minimax optimal rate regardless of the scaling law of the teacher -- in fact, when the test error of the teacher does not even decay with the sample size.

</details>


### [134] [Revisiting the (Sub)Optimality of Best-of-N for Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05739)
*Ved Sriraman,Adam Block*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了Best-of-N采样方法，发现在实际条件下（使用胜率而非期望奖励作为指标），适当调优的BoN在统计和计算上都是最优的，并提出了一个能消除奖励黑客攻击的实用变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Best-of-N采样在实践中被广泛使用，但先前理论研究认为它在统计上不是最优的且容易受到奖励黑客攻击。然而，这些分析基于期望真实奖励的假设，与实践中奖励模型的训练和评估方式（基于成对比较的胜率）不符，因此需要重新评估BoN在实际条件下的表现。

Method: 1. 在更贴近实践的假设下分析BoN采样：使用胜率而非期望奖励作为评估指标；2. 证明在参考模型和奖励模型质量满足最小条件时，适当调优的BoN在统计和计算上都是最优的；3. 提出一个简单的BoN变体，能证明性地消除奖励黑客攻击同时保持最优统计性能；4. 对比分析先前方法在胜率指标下的次优性。

Result: 1. 在胜率指标下，适当调优的BoN采样在统计和计算上都是最优的，这解释了其广泛的实际成功；2. 提出的BoN变体能有效消除奖励黑客攻击问题；3. 先前分析方法在考虑胜率时被证明是次优的，突显了选择适当目标函数的重要性。

Conclusion: Best-of-N采样在实际条件下（使用胜率指标）是统计和计算最优的推理时对齐方法，其广泛成功有理论依据。通过提出的简单变体可以消除奖励黑客攻击问题。研究强调了在分析推理时对齐方法时选择适当目标函数的重要性，胜率比期望奖励更能反映实际应用场景。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is a widely used inference-time alignment method for language models, whereby N candidate responses are sampled from a reference model and the one with the highest predicted reward according to a learned reward model is selected. Despite its widespread practical use, recent theoretical work has suggested that it is statistically suboptimal and vulnerable to reward hacking, the process by which models exploit weaknesses in the learned reward model to achieve high estimated reward without genuinely improving performance. We revisit this question under assumptions that more closely reflect practice than that of prior work. In particular, in contradistinction to earlier analyses that focused on expected true reward, which may not be meaningful in many practical settings, we investigate how inference-time alignment affects the win-rate, a pairwise comparison-based metric more closely aligned with how reward models are trained and evaluated in practice. We demonstrate that, under minimal conditions on the quality of the reference model and learned reward model, properly tuned BoN is both computationally and statistically optimal in achieving high win-rate, partially explaining its widespread practical success. Because BoN remains susceptible to reward-hacking in this setting, we propose a simple and practical variant that provably eliminates reward-hacking while maintaining optimal statistical performance. Finally, we show that prior approaches are provably suboptimal when considering win-rate, highlighting the importance of choosing appropriate objectives when analyzing inference-time alignment methods.

</details>


### [135] [MIRACL: A Diverse Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Objective Multi-Echelon Combinatorial Supply Chain Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05760)
*Rifny Rachman,Josh Tingey,Richard Allmendinger,Wei Pan,Pradyumn Shukla,Bahrul Ilmi Nasution*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIRACL是一个分层元多目标强化学习框架，通过任务分解和Pareto自适应策略实现少样本泛化，在组合优化问题中优于传统MORL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标强化学习在动态环境中部署面临任务特定重训练和计算成本高的问题，需要能够快速适应新任务的通用框架。

Method: 提出MIRACL框架：1) 将任务分解为结构化子问题以高效策略适应；2) 使用基于Pareto的自适应策略跨任务元学习全局策略，促进元训练和微调中的多样性。

Result: 在简单到中等复杂度的任务中，MIRACL优于传统MORL基线，实现了高达10%的超体积提升和5%的期望效用改进。

Conclusion: MIRACL展示了在多目标问题中实现鲁棒高效适应的潜力，虽然是针对供应链领域验证，但理论上具有领域无关性，适用于更广泛的动态多目标决策问题。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL) is effective for multi-echelon combinatorial supply chain optimisation, where tasks involve high dimensionality, uncertainty, and competing objectives. However, its deployment in dynamic environments is hindered by the need for task-specific retraining and substantial computational cost. We introduce MIRACL (Meta multI-objective Reinforcement leArning with Composite Learning), a hierarchical Meta-MORL framework that allows for a few-shot generalisation across diverse tasks. MIRACL decomposes each task into structured subproblems for efficient policy adaptation and meta-learns a global policy across tasks using a Pareto-based adaptation strategy to encourage diversity in meta-training and fine-tuning. To our knowledge, this is the first integration of Meta-MORL with such mechanisms in combinatorial optimisation. Although validated in the supply chain domain, MIRACL is theoretically domain-agnostic and applicable to broader dynamic multi-objective decision-making problems. Empirical evaluations show that MIRACL outperforms conventional MORL baselines in simple to moderate tasks, achieving up to 10% higher hypervolume and 5% better expected utility. These results underscore the potential of MIRACL for robust, efficient adaptation in multi-objective problems.

</details>


### [136] [Score-Guided Proximal Projection: A Unified Geometric Framework for Rectified Flow Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05761)
*Vansh Bansal,James G Scott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Score-Guided Proximal Projection (SGPP)框架，解决Rectified Flow模型在精确控制任务中的局限性，通过近端优化平衡输入保真度和预训练分数场的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rectified Flow模型虽然生成质量优秀，但在精确控制任务（如语义编辑或盲图像恢复）中存在挑战。现有方法分为两类：基于反转的引导方法存在"几何锁定"问题，严格遵循源轨迹；后验采样近似方法（如DPS）计算成本高且不稳定。需要一种统一框架来弥合确定性优化和随机采样之间的差距。

Method: 提出Score-Guided Proximal Projection (SGPP)框架，将恢复任务重新表述为近端优化问题，定义了一个平衡输入保真度和预训练分数场真实性的能量景观。该方法具有正态收缩特性，能保证分布外输入被"捕捉"到数据流形上，并有效达到流形约束下的后验模式。SGPP通过放松近端方差实现"软引导"，提供严格身份保持和生成自由度之间的连续权衡。

Result: SGPP统一了最先进的编辑方法：RF-inversion是其框架的极限情况。该方法提供了训练自由的连续权衡，在保持身份和生成自由度之间实现灵活控制。理论上证明了该目标具有正态收缩特性，几何上保证分布外输入被映射到数据流形。

Conclusion: SGPP是一个统一的框架，弥合了确定性优化和随机采样之间的差距，解决了Rectified Flow模型在精确控制任务中的局限性。它通过近端优化平衡保真度和真实性，提供灵活的"软引导"机制，为图像编辑和恢复任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Rectified Flow (RF) models achieve state-of-the-art generation quality, yet controlling them for precise tasks -- such as semantic editing or blind image recovery -- remains a challenge. Current approaches bifurcate into inversion-based guidance, which suffers from "geometric locking" by rigidly adhering to the source trajectory, and posterior sampling approximations (e.g., DPS), which are computationally expensive and unstable. In this work, we propose Score-Guided Proximal Projection (SGPP), a unified framework that bridges the gap between deterministic optimization and stochastic sampling. We reformulate the recovery task as a proximal optimization problem, defining an energy landscape that balances fidelity to the input with realism from the pre-trained score field. We theoretically prove that this objective induces a normal contraction property, geometrically guaranteeing that out-of-distribution inputs are snapped onto the data manifold, and it effectively reaches the posterior mode constrained to the manifold. Crucially, we demonstrate that SGPP generalizes state-of-the-art editing methods: RF-inversion is effectively a limiting case of our framework. By relaxing the proximal variance, SGPP enables "soft guidance," offering a continuous, training-free trade-off between strict identity preservation and generative freedom.

</details>


### [137] [TML-Bench: Benchmark for Data Science Agents on Tabular ML Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05764)
*Mykola Pinchuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TML-Bench是一个用于评估数据科学智能体在Kaggle风格表格任务上表现的基准测试，通过测试10个开源大语言模型在4个Kaggle竞赛和3个时间预算下的表现，发现MiniMax-M2.1模型在综合性能上表现最佳，且平均性能随时间预算增加而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然自主编码智能体能够在Kaggle风格任务上快速生成强大的表格基线，但其实际价值取决于端到端的正确性和时间限制下的可靠性。因此需要建立一个专门的基准测试来评估数据科学智能体在真实场景中的表现。

Method: 1. 构建TML-Bench基准测试，包含4个Kaggle竞赛任务；2. 评估10个开源大语言模型；3. 设置3个时间预算（240秒、600秒、1200秒）；4. 每个模型在每个任务和时间预算下运行5次；5. 成功标准：生成有效提交并在隐藏标签上获得私有保留集分数；6. 报告中位数性能、成功率和运行间变异性。

Result: 1. MiniMax-M2.1模型在所有四个竞赛中获得了最佳综合性能分数；2. 平均性能随着时间预算的增加而提高；3. 在当前运行次数下，某些个体模型的扩展存在噪声；4. 提供了详细的性能指标和变异性分析。

Conclusion: TML-Bench为评估数据科学智能体在表格任务上的表现提供了一个标准化基准，证明了自主编码智能体在Kaggle风格任务中的潜力，同时揭示了时间预算对性能的影响以及模型稳定性的重要性。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents can produce strong tabular baselines quickly on Kaggle-style tasks. Practical value depends on end-to-end correctness and reliability under time limits. This paper introduces TML-Bench, a tabular benchmark for data science agents on Kaggle-style tasks. This paper evaluates 10 OSS LLMs on four Kaggle competitions and three time budgets (240s, 600s, and 1200s). Each model is run five times per task and budget. A run is successful if it produces a valid submission and a private-holdout score on hidden labels that are not accessible to the agent. This paper reports median performance, success rates, and run-to-run variability. MiniMax-M2.1 model achieves the best aggregate performance score on all four competitions under the paper's primary aggregation. Average performance improves with larger time budgets. Scaling is noisy for some individual models at the current run count. Code and materials are available at https://github.com/MykolaPinchuk/TML-bench/tree/master.

</details>


### [138] [Bridging Domains through Subspace-Aware Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05768)
*Levy Chaves,Chao Zhou,Rebekka Burkholz,Eduardo Valle,Sandra Avila*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCORE方法通过解决子空间冲突来提升模型合并的领域泛化能力，在多种架构和规模上优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型合并研究主要关注同分布或多任务场景，但领域泛化方面的模型合并研究不足。本文旨在探索在不同领域微调的模型合并后对未见领域的泛化能力，并解决由此产生的参数竞争问题。

Method: 提出SCORE（Subspace COnflict-Resolving mErging）方法：1）通过计算所有模型主导奇异向量的主成分来寻找共享正交基；2）将每个任务矩阵投影到共享基上；3）修剪非对角分量以移除冲突的奇异方向。

Result: SCORE在领域泛化设置中平均优于现有模型合并方法，在多种架构和模型规模上表现出有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 模型合并中的领域泛化存在独特的子空间冲突问题，SCORE通过解决这些冲突显著提升了合并模型在未见领域的泛化性能，为模型合并的领域泛化研究提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Model merging integrates multiple task-specific models into a single consolidated one. Recent research has made progress in improving merging performance for in-distribution or multi-task scenarios, but domain generalization in model merging remains underexplored. We investigate how merging models fine-tuned on distinct domains affects generalization to unseen domains. Through an analysis of parameter competition in the task matrix using singular value decomposition, we show that merging models trained under different distribution shifts induces stronger conflicts between their subspaces compared to traditional multi-task settings. To mitigate this issue, we propose SCORE (Subspace COnflict-Resolving mErging), a method designed to alleviate such singular subspace conflicts. SCORE finds a shared orthogonal basis by computing the principal components of the concatenated leading singular vectors of all models. It then projects each task matrix into the shared basis, pruning off-diagonal components to remove conflicting singular directions. SCORE consistently outperforms, on average, existing model merging approaches in domain generalization settings across a variety of architectures and model scales, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [139] [First-Order Softmax Weighted Switching Gradient Method for Distributed Stochastic Minimax Optimization with Stochastic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05774)
*Zhankun Luo,Antesh Upadhyay,Sang Bin Moon,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于联邦学习的Softmax加权切换梯度方法，解决带随机约束的分布式随机极小极大优化问题，在完全和部分客户端参与下均获得收敛保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决联邦学习中带随机约束的分布式随机极小极大优化问题，传统原始-对偶或基于惩罚的方法存在超参数敏感性和收敛振荡问题，需要更稳定的优化框架

Method: 提出Softmax加权切换梯度方法，采用单循环原始切换机制，通过放松目标函数有界性假设，建立更严格的softmax超参数下界，提供统一的误差分解

Result: 在完全客户端参与下达到标准O(ε⁻⁴)的oracle复杂度，满足最优性间隙和可行性容忍度的统一界ε；在部分参与下通过随机优势假设量化客户端采样噪声；建立O(log(1/δ))的高概率收敛保证

Conclusion: 单循环原始切换机制为优化最坏情况客户端性能提供了稳定替代方案，有效规避传统方法的超参数敏感性和收敛振荡问题，在NP分类和公平分类任务中验证了有效性

Abstract: This paper addresses the distributed stochastic minimax optimization problem subject to stochastic constraints. We propose a novel first-order Softmax-Weighted Switching Gradient method tailored for federated learning. Under full client participation, our algorithm achieves the standard $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ oracle complexity to satisfy a unified bound $ε$ for both the optimality gap and feasibility tolerance. We extend our theoretical analysis to the practical partial participation regime by quantifying client sampling noise through a stochastic superiority assumption. Furthermore, by relaxing standard boundedness assumptions on the objective functions, we establish a strictly tighter lower bound for the softmax hyperparameter. We provide a unified error decomposition and establish a sharp $\mathcal{O}(\log\frac{1}δ)$ high-probability convergence guarantee. Ultimately, our framework demonstrates that a single-loop primal-only switching mechanism provides a stable alternative for optimizing worst-case client performance, effectively bypassing the hyperparameter sensitivity and convergence oscillations often encountered in traditional primal-dual or penalty-based approaches. We verify the efficacy of our algorithm via experiment on the Neyman-Pearson (NP) classification and fair classification tasks.

</details>


### [140] [Sparse Crosscoders for diffing MoEs and Dense models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05805)
*Marmik Chaudhari,Nishkal Hundia,Idhant Gulati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过交叉编码器系统比较MoE和密集模型内部表示，发现MoE学习更少但更专业化的特征，而密集模型具有更广泛、通用的特征分布


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MoE通过稀疏专家路由实现了参数高效扩展，但其内部表示相比密集模型仍缺乏深入理解，需要系统比较两者内部特征组织差异

Method: 使用交叉编码器（稀疏自编码器变体）联合建模多个激活空间，训练5层密集和MoE模型（等量活跃参数）于10亿token的代码、科学文本和英文故事数据，采用BatchTopK交叉编码器并显式指定共享特征

Result: 获得约87%的分数方差解释率，MoE学习显著更少的独特特征；MoE特定特征比共享特征具有更高的激活密度，而密集模型特定特征密度较低；MoE发展出更专业化、集中的表示，密集模型则在更广泛、通用的特征间分布信息

Conclusion: MoE通过更少的独特特征实现更专业化的表示，而密集模型采用更广泛的特征分布策略，这揭示了两种架构在特征组织上的根本差异

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) achieve parameter-efficient scaling through sparse expert routing, yet their internal representations remain poorly understood compared to dense models. We present a systematic comparison of MoE and dense model internals using crosscoders, a variant of sparse autoencoders, that jointly models multiple activation spaces. We train 5-layer dense and MoEs (equal active parameters) on 1B tokens across code, scientific text, and english stories. Using BatchTopK crosscoders with explicitly designated shared features, we achieve $\sim 87\%$ fractional variance explained and uncover concrete differences in feature organization. The MoE learns significantly fewer unique features compared to the dense model. MoE-specific features also exhibit higher activation density than shared features, whereas dense-specific features show lower density. Our analysis reveals that MoEs develop more specialized, focused representations while dense models distribute information across broader, more general-purpose features.

</details>


### [141] [MoE Lens -- An Expert Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05806)
*Marmik Chaudhari,Idhant Gulati,Nishkal Hundia,Pranav Karra,Shivam Raval*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE模型专家专业化分析显示，尽管有64个路由专家，模型主要依赖少数专家，单个顶级专家的输出接近完整集成预测，为推理优化提供机会


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型通过稀疏专家激活实现参数高效扩展，但对其专业化行为的理解有限，阻碍了推理和内存成本的优化

Method: 采用两种互补方法：领域特定路由模式和早期解码框架，跟踪专家对输出表示的贡献；分析DeepSeekMoE模型的路由分布和隐藏状态相似性

Result: 模型主要依赖少数专业化专家，顶级专家的输出与完整集成预测高度相似；少数专家处理超过50%的路由决策；单专家与集成专家的隐藏状态相似度极高（余弦相似度达0.95），使用单专家时困惑度仅增加5%

Conclusion: MoE模型表现出集中的专业知识，为通过有针对性的专家剪枝进行推理优化提供了机会，同时保持模型性能，并为研究这些模型中学习知识的定位开辟了途径

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) models enable parameter-efficient scaling through sparse expert activations, yet optimizing their inference and memory costs remains challenging due to limited understanding of their specialization behavior. We present a systematic analysis of expert specialization in MoEs through two complementary approaches: domain-specific routing patterns and an early decoding framework that tracks expert contributions to output representations. Our analysis of the DeepSeekMoE model reveals that despite having 64 routed experts with 6 active for each layer's computation, the model predominantly relies on a few specialized experts, with the top-weighted expert's output closely approximating the full ensemble prediction. We quantitatively validate these findings through a systematic analysis of the token routing distribution, demonstrating that very few experts handle over 50\% of routing decisions across different specialized domains. Hidden state similarity between single and ensemble experts for every layer is extremely high, with some layers having cosine similarity as high as 0.95 and perplexity increasing by only 5\% when using a single expert across all three domains. Our results indicate that Mixture of Experts models exhibit concentrated expertise highlighting potential opportunities for inference optimization through targeted expert pruning while maintaining model performance and opening avenues towards studying localization of learned knowledge in these models.

</details>


### [142] [Self-Auditing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Few-Shot 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05822)
*Son Thai Ly,Hien V. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEA-PEFT是一种自动化参数高效微调方法，通过搜索-审计-分配循环动态配置适配器，在少样本3D医学图像分割中显著优于固定拓扑PEFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在临床站点适应面临挑战：领域偏移、标注稀缺、缺乏AI工程师进行适配器调优，导致适应周期长达数周至数月。现有PEFT方法需要手动配置适配器或计算成本高昂的自动搜索，在少样本3D场景中不可行。

Method: SEA-PEFT将适配器配置视为在线分配问题，采用搜索-审计-分配循环：训练活动适配器，通过瞬时关闭每个适配器估计其Dice效用，在参数预算下使用贪心背包分配器重新选择活动集。结合指数移动平均、四分位距平滑和有限状态排名控制器稳定循环，提高高噪声少样本场景的可靠性。

Result: 在TotalSegmentator和FLARE'22数据集上，SEA-PEFT在1/5/10-shot设置中比最强固定拓扑PEFT基线平均Dice提升2.4-2.8个百分点，同时训练参数少于1%。

Conclusion: SEA-PEFT通过自动化适配器配置解决了临床站点适应的关键瓶颈，显著减少了人工干预需求，在少样本3D医学图像分割中实现了高效可靠的模型适应。

Abstract: Adapting foundation models to new clinical sites remains challenging in practice. Domain shift and scarce annotations must be handled by experts, yet many clinical groups do not have ready access to skilled AI engineers to tune adapter designs and training recipes. As a result, adaptation cycles can stretch from weeks to months, particularly in few-shot settings. Existing PEFT methods either require manual adapter configuration or automated searches that are computationally infeasible in few-shot 3D settings. We propose SEA-PEFT (SElf-Auditing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning) to automate this process. SEA-PEFT treats adapter configuration as an online allocation problem solved during fine-tuning rather than through manual, fixed-topology choices. SEA-PEFT uses a search-audit-allocate loop that trains active adapters, estimates each adapter's Dice utility by momentarily toggling it off, and then reselects the active set under a parameter budget using a greedy knapsack allocator. Exponential Moving Average and Interquartile Range smoothing, together with a Finite-State Ranking controller, stabilize the loop and improve reliability in high-noise few-shot regimes. On TotalSegmentator and FLARE'22, SEA-PEFT improves mean Dice by 2.4--2.8 points over the strongest fixed-topology PEFT baselines across 1/5/10-shot settings while training <1% of parameters. For reproducibility purposes, we made our code publicly available at https://github.com/tsly123/SEA_PEFT

</details>


### [143] [Test-Time Adaptation via Many-Shot Prompting: Benefits, Limits, and Pitfalls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05829)
*Shubhangi Upasani,Chen Wu,Jay Rainton,Bo Li,Changran Hu,Qizheng Zhang,Urmish Thakker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文实证研究了大型语言模型在测试时通过多示例提示进行自适应的方法，分析了不同任务和模型架构下性能随更新规模、示例顺序和选择策略的变化，并探讨了动态和强化ICL作为替代策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应使LLMs能在推理时修改行为而不更新模型参数，多示例提示是常见方法，但这种方法在开源模型中的可靠性和限制尚不清楚，需要系统研究其实际效果和边界。

Method: 通过实证研究分析多示例提示在不同任务和模型架构下的表现，考察性能随更新规模（示例数量）、示例顺序和选择策略的变化，并研究动态ICL和强化ICL作为替代测试时更新策略。

Result: 多示例提示对结构化任务（演示提供高信息增益）有效，但对选择策略高度敏感，在开放式生成任务中收益有限；动态和强化ICL能控制信息注入方式和约束模型行为。

Conclusion: 系统刻画了基于提示的测试时自适应的实际限制，明确了输入空间更新在何种情况下有益或有害，为实际应用提供了指导。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation enables large language models (LLMs) to modify their behavior at inference without updating model parameters. A common approach is many-shot prompting, where large numbers of in-context learning (ICL) examples are injected as an input-space test-time update. Although performance can improve as more demonstrations are added, the reliability and limits of this update mechanism remain poorly understood, particularly for open-source models. We present an empirical study of many-shot prompting across tasks and model backbones, analyzing how performance varies with update magnitude, example ordering, and selection policy. We further study Dynamic and Reinforced ICL as alternative test-time update strategies that control which information is injected and how it constrains model behavior. We find that many-shot prompting is effective for structured tasks where demonstrations provide high information gain, but is highly sensitive to selection strategy and often shows limited benefits for open-ended generation tasks. Overall, we characterize the practical limits of prompt-based test-time adaptation and outline when input-space updates are beneficial versus harmful.

</details>


### [144] [Reference-guided Policy Optimization for Molecular Optimization via LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05900)
*Xuan Li,Zhanke Zhou,Zongze Li,Jiangchao Yao,Yu Rong,Lu Zhang,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RePO是一种用于分子优化的新方法，结合了强化学习和参考指导，解决了传统SFT和RLVR在分子优化任务中的局限性，无需轨迹数据即可有效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督微调(SFT)和带可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)在分子优化任务中表现不佳，因为每个数据点通常只提供一个优化的参考分子，没有逐步优化轨迹。仅答案的SFT会导致推理能力崩溃，而RLVR在相似性约束下由于模型缺乏有效探索而提供稀疏反馈，限制了优化效果。

Method: 提出Reference-guided Policy Optimization (RePO)方法：1) 从模型中采样候选分子及其中间推理轨迹；2) 使用可验证奖励在强化学习框架下训练模型，奖励衡量相似性约束下的属性满足度；3) 应用参考指导，将策略的中间推理轨迹作为上下文，仅以监督方式训练答案部分。RL项促进探索，指导项通过将输出锚定到参考分子来缓解奖励稀疏性并稳定训练。

Result: 在分子优化基准测试中，RePO始终优于SFT和RLVR基线(如GRPO)，在优化指标(成功率×相似性)上取得改进，更好地平衡了竞争目标，并对未见过的指令风格具有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: RePO通过结合强化学习的探索能力和参考指导的稳定性，有效解决了分子优化中缺乏轨迹数据的问题，为指令驱动的分子优化提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) benefit substantially from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) in reasoning tasks. However, these recipes perform poorly in instruction-based molecular optimization, where each data point typically provides only a single optimized reference molecule and no step-by-step optimization trajectory. We reveal that answer-only SFT on the reference molecules collapses reasoning, and RLVR provides sparse feedback under similarity constraints due to the model's lack of effective exploration, which slows learning and limits optimization. To encourage the exploration of new molecules while balancing the exploitation of the reference molecules, we introduce Reference-guided Policy Optimization (RePO), an optimization approach that learns from reference molecules without requiring trajectory data. At each update, RePO samples candidate molecules with their intermediate reasoning trajectories from the model and trains the model using verifiable rewards that measure property satisfaction under similarity constraints in an RL manner. Meanwhile, it applies reference guidance by keeping the policy's intermediate reasoning trajectory as context and training only the answer in a supervised manner. Together, the RL term promotes exploration, while the guidance term mitigates reward sparsity and stabilizes training by grounding outputs to references when many valid molecular edits exist. Across molecular optimization benchmarks, RePO consistently outperforms SFT and RLVR baselines (e.g., GRPO), achieving improvements on the optimization metric (Success Rate $\times$ Similarity), improving balance across competing objectives, and generalizing better to unseen instruction styles. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/RePO.

</details>


### [145] [Design Experiments to Compare Multi-armed Bandit Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05919)
*Huiling Meng,Ningyuan Chen,Xuefeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Artificial Replay（AR）实验设计，用于在线平台比较多臂老虎机算法，通过重用第一个策略的记录来减少实验成本，实现无偏估计和亚线性方差增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台需要比较UCB、Thompson Sampling等多臂老虎机算法，但传统方法需要多次独立重启算法，导致实验成本高且部署决策延迟。每个算法运行产生一个依赖轨迹，需要可靠推断。

Method: 提出Artificial Replay（AR）实验设计：首先运行一个策略并记录其轨迹；当执行第二个策略时，如果选择第一个策略已经采取过的动作，则重用记录奖励，否则查询真实环境。开发新的分析框架证明该估计器的性质。

Result: AR估计器具有三个关键性质：1）无偏；2）仅需T+o(T)用户交互而非2T，当两个策略都有亚线性遗憾时几乎减半实验成本；3）方差亚线性增长，而朴素设计的估计器方差线性增长。数值实验验证了理论优势。

Conclusion: AR为多臂老虎机算法比较提供了一种高效实验设计，显著降低实验成本，加速部署决策，同时保证统计可靠性。

Abstract: Online platforms routinely compare multi-armed bandit algorithms, such as UCB and Thompson Sampling, to select the best-performing policy. Unlike standard A/B tests for static treatments, each run of a bandit algorithm over $T$ users produces only one dependent trajectory, because the algorithm's decisions depend on all past interactions. Reliable inference therefore demands many independent restarts of the algorithm, making experimentation costly and delaying deployment decisions. We propose Artificial Replay (AR) as a new experimental design for this problem. AR first runs one policy and records its trajectory. When the second policy is executed, it reuses a recorded reward whenever it selects an action the first policy already took, and queries the real environment only otherwise. We develop a new analytical framework for this design and prove three key properties of the resulting estimator: it is unbiased; it requires only $T + o(T)$ user interactions instead of $2T$ for a run of the treatment and control policies, nearly halving the experimental cost when both policies have sub-linear regret; and its variance grows sub-linearly in $T$, whereas the estimator from a naïve design has a linearly-growing variance. Numerical experiments with UCB, Thompson Sampling, and $ε$-greedy policies confirm these theoretical gains.

</details>


### [146] [Omni-Masked Gradient Descent: Memory-Efficient Optimization via Mask Traversal with Improved Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05960)
*Hui Yang,Tao Ren,Jinyang Jiang,Wan Tian,Yijie Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OMGD是一种基于掩码遍历的内存高效优化方法，在非凸设置下实现了改进的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$迭代复杂度，优于标准的${\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模语言模型全参数训练中的GPU内存瓶颈问题。现有方法要么缺乏清晰的收敛保证，要么在非凸设置下只能达到标准的${\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$迭代复杂度。

Method: 提出Omni-Masked Gradient Descent (OMGD)，一种基于掩码遍历的内存高效优化方法。该方法轻量级、即插即用，可无缝集成到大多数主流优化器中。

Result: 理论分析建立了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$的改进迭代复杂度，用于寻找$ε$-近似驻点。实证结果表明，在微调和预训练任务中，OMGD相比竞争基线都能带来一致的改进。

Conclusion: OMGD是一种有效的内存高效优化方法，在理论收敛保证和实际性能方面都优于现有方法，为大规模语言模型训练提供了更好的解决方案。

Abstract: Memory-efficient optimization methods have recently gained increasing attention for scaling full-parameter training of large language models under the GPU-memory bottleneck. Existing approaches either lack clear convergence guarantees, or only achieve the standard ${\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ iteration complexity in the nonconvex settings. We propose Omni-Masked Gradient Descent (OMGD), an optimization method based on mask traversal for memory efficient training, and provide a nonconvex convergence analysis that establishes a strictly improved iteration complexity of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$ for finding an $ε$-approximate stationary point. Empirically, OMGD is a lightweight, plug-and-play approach that integrates seamlessly into most mainstream optimizers, yielding consistent improvements over competitive baselines in both fine-tuning and pre-training tasks.

</details>


### [147] [Preventing Learning Stagnation in PPO by Scaling to 1 Million Parallel Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06009)
*Michael Beukman,Khimya Khetarpal,Zeyu Zheng,Will Dabney,Jakob Foerster,Michael Dennis,Clare Lyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PPO性能停滞问题源于样本估计损失与真实目标间的偏差，可通过增加并行环境数量减少梯度噪声和步长来解决


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度在线策略强化学习中普遍存在的性能停滞问题，特别是在PPO中，这种停滞并非源于已知的探索、容量或优化挑战，而是因为样本估计的损失在训练过程中逐渐偏离真实目标

Method: 将PPO的外循环建模为随机优化问题，分析步长（由正则化强度控制）和梯度噪声（由更新间收集的样本数决定）的关系。提出通过增加并行环境数量来同时减少步长和噪声，并提供超参数协同缩放方案

Result: 验证了模型预测，发现增加并行环境数量能有效解决性能停滞问题。通过将PPO扩展到超过100万个并行环境，在复杂开放领域大幅超越先前基线，实现单调性能提升直至一万亿次状态转移

Conclusion: PPO中的性能停滞源于外循环中步长与梯度噪声的不匹配，增加并行环境数量是解决该问题的简单有效方法，正确的超参数协同缩放能实现大规模单调性能提升

Abstract: Plateaus, where an agent's performance stagnates at a suboptimal level, are a common problem in deep on-policy RL. Focusing on PPO due to its widespread adoption, we show that plateaus in certain regimes arise not because of known exploration, capacity, or optimization challenges, but because sample-based estimates of the loss eventually become poor proxies for the true objective over the course of training. As a recap, PPO switches between sampling rollouts from several parallel environments online using the current policy (which we call the outer loop) and performing repeated minibatch SGD steps against this offline dataset (the inner loop). In our work we consider only the outer loop, and conceptually model it as stochastic optimization. The step size is then controlled by the regularization strength towards the previous policy and the gradient noise by the number of samples collected between policy update steps. This model predicts that performance will plateau at a suboptimal level if the outer step size is too large relative to the noise. Recasting PPO in this light makes it clear that there are two ways to address this particular type of learning stagnation: either reduce the step size or increase the number of samples collected between updates. We first validate the predictions of our model and investigate how hyperparameter choices influence the step size and update noise, concluding that increasing the number of parallel environments is a simple and robust way to reduce both factors. Next, we propose a recipe for how to co-scale the other hyperparameters when increasing parallelization, and show that incorrectly doing so can lead to severe performance degradation. Finally, we vastly outperform prior baselines in a complex open-ended domain by scaling PPO to more than 1M parallel environments, thereby enabling monotonic performance improvement up to one trillion transitions.

</details>


### [148] [Agnostic learning in (almost) optimal time via Gaussian surface area](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06027)
*Lucas Pesenti,Lucas Slot,Manuel Wiedmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进了Klivans等人(2008)关于高斯分布下概念类学习复杂度的分析，将所需的多项式次数从O(Γ²/ε⁴)降低到Õ(Γ²/ε²)，从而在统计查询模型中获得了（近似）最优的不可知学习多项式阈值函数复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯分布下概念类在困难不可知模型中的学习复杂度与其通过低次多项式的L₁近似能力密切相关。Klivans等人(2008)证明了对于高斯表面积至多为Γ的概念类，次数d=O(Γ²/ε⁴)足以实现ε近似，这为多种概念类的学习提供了最佳已知界限。本文旨在改进这一分析以获得更紧的界限。

Method: 本文改进了Klivans等人(2008)的分析方法，通过使用与Feldman等人(2020)在布尔超立方体上L₁近似构造直接类似的技术，证明了次数d=Õ(Γ²/ε²)就足以实现ε近似。

Result: 主要结果是显著改进了所需多项式次数的界限：从O(Γ²/ε⁴)降低到Õ(Γ²/ε²)。结合Diakonikolas等人(2021)的下界，这为统计查询模型中不可知学习多项式阈值函数的复杂度提供了（近似）最优的界限。

Conclusion: 本文通过改进分析技术，显著降低了高斯分布下概念类L₁近似所需的多项式次数，从而在统计查询模型中获得了不可知学习多项式阈值函数的（近似）最优复杂度界限，这一改进基于与布尔超立方体上类似构造的直接类比。

Abstract: The complexity of learning a concept class under Gaussian marginals in the difficult agnostic model is closely related to its $L_1$-approximability by low-degree polynomials. For any concept class with Gaussian surface area at most $Γ$, Klivans et al. (2008) show that degree $d = O(Γ^2 / \varepsilon^4)$ suffices to achieve an $\varepsilon$-approximation. This leads to the best-known bounds on the complexity of learning a variety of concept classes. In this note, we improve their analysis by showing that degree $d = \tilde O (Γ^2 / \varepsilon^2)$ is enough. In light of lower bounds due to Diakonikolas et al. (2021), this yields (near) optimal bounds on the complexity of agnostically learning polynomial threshold functions in the statistical query model. Our proof relies on a direct analogue of a construction of Feldman et al. (2020), who considered $L_1$-approximation on the Boolean hypercube.

</details>


### [149] [Improved high-dimensional estimation with Langevin dynamics and stochastic weight averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06028)
*Stanley Wei,Alex Damian,Jason D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了Langevin动力学通过迭代平均而非最终迭代，在样本复杂度n ≳ d^{k*/2}下恢复隐藏方向θ*的能力，这达到了与显式平滑方法相同的速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，在线SGD和Langevin动力学需要n ≳ d^{max(1, k*-1)}样本才能恢复隐藏方向θ*，而通过显式平滑的梯度下降可以将样本复杂度降低到n ≳ d^{max(1, k*/2)}。这引发了一个问题：是否可以在不进行显式平滑的情况下达到相同的速率？

Method: 采用Langevin动力学（噪声注入的梯度下降），但考虑平均迭代而非最终迭代。理论分析表明，噪声注入和迭代平均的组合能够模拟景观平滑的效果。

Result: 证明Langevin动力学在考虑平均迭代时，能够在样本复杂度n ≳ d^{k*/2}下成功恢复隐藏方向θ*，达到了与显式平滑方法相同的速率。该结果适用于张量PCA和单指标模型设置。

Conclusion: 噪声注入和迭代平均的组合能够有效模拟景观平滑的效果，使Langevin动力学在不进行显式平滑的情况下达到最优样本复杂度。作者还推测小批量SGD也能在不添加额外噪声的情况下达到相同速率。

Abstract: Significant recent work has studied the ability of gradient descent to recover a hidden planted direction $θ^\star \in S^{d-1}$ in different high-dimensional settings, including tensor PCA and single-index models. The key quantity that governs the ability of gradient descent to traverse these landscapes is the information exponent $k^\star$ (Ben Arous et al., (2021)), which corresponds to the order of the saddle at initialization in the population landscape. Ben Arous et al., (2021) showed that $n \gtrsim d^{\max(1, k^\star-1)}$ samples were necessary and sufficient for online SGD to recover $θ^\star$, and Ben Arous et al., (2020) proved a similar lower bound for Langevin dynamics. More recently, Damian et al., (2023) showed it was possible to circumvent these lower bounds by running gradient descent on a smoothed landscape, and that this algorithm succeeds with $n \gtrsim d^{\max(1, k^\star/2)}$ samples, which is optimal in the worst case. This raises the question of whether it is possible to achieve the same rate without explicit smoothing. In this paper, we show that Langevin dynamics can succeed with $n \gtrsim d^{ k^\star/2 }$ samples if one considers the average iterate, rather than the last iterate. The key idea is that the combination of noise-injection and iterate averaging is able to emulate the effect of landscape smoothing. We apply this result to both the tensor PCA and single-index model settings. Finally, we conjecture that minibatch SGD can also achieve the same rate without adding any additional noise.

</details>


### [150] [Dynamic Momentum Recalibration in Online Gradient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06120)
*Zhipeng Yao,Rui Yu,Guisong Chang,Ying Li,Yu Zhang,Dazhou Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SGDF优化器，通过最优线性滤波原理动态调整梯度估计，在噪声抑制与信号保留间取得最优权衡，超越传统动量方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD及其动量变体是深度学习优化的核心，但其梯度行为的底层动力学机制理解不足。固定动量系数会固有地扭曲偏差与方差之间的平衡，导致参数更新偏斜或次优

Method: 提出SGDF（带滤波器的SGD），受最优线性滤波原理启发。计算在线时变增益，通过最小化均方误差动态优化梯度估计，实现噪声抑制与信号保留的最优权衡。该方法可扩展到其他优化器

Result: 在多种架构和基准测试上的广泛实验表明，SGDF超越了传统动量方法，性能达到或超过了最先进的优化器

Conclusion: 通过信号处理视角重新解释梯度更新，SGDF提供了一种动态调整梯度估计的方法，在优化框架中具有广泛适用性，显著提升了优化性能

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) and its momentum variants form the backbone of deep learning optimization, yet the underlying dynamics of their gradient behavior remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we reinterpret gradient updates through the lens of signal processing and reveal that fixed momentum coefficients inherently distort the balance between bias and variance, leading to skewed or suboptimal parameter updates. To address this, we propose SGDF (SGD with Filter), an optimizer inspired by the principles of Optimal Linear Filtering. SGDF computes an online, time-varying gain to dynamically refine gradient estimation by minimizing the mean-squared error, thereby achieving an optimal trade-off between noise suppression and signal preservation. Furthermore, our approach could extend to other optimizers, showcasing its broad applicability to optimization frameworks. Extensive experiments across diverse architectures and benchmarks demonstrate SGDF surpasses conventional momentum methods and achieves performance on par with or surpassing state-of-the-art optimizers.

</details>


### [151] [DQE: A Semantic-Aware Evaluation Metric for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06131)
*Yuewei Li,Dalin Zhang,Huan Li,Xinyi Gong,Hongjun Chu,Zhaohui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对时间序列异常检测的评估问题，提出了一种基于检测语义的新评估指标，解决了现有指标在点级覆盖偏差、近误检测不敏感、误报惩罚不足和阈值选择不一致等方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测评估指标存在多个严重缺陷：偏向点级覆盖、对近误检测不敏感或不一致、对误报惩罚不足、以及由阈值或阈值区间选择导致的不一致性。这些限制会产生不可靠或反直觉的结果，阻碍客观进展。

Method: 1. 提出基于检测语义的分区策略，将每个异常的局部时间区域分解为三个功能不同的子区域；2. 基于此分区评估跨事件的整体检测行为，并为每个子区域设计更细粒度的评分机制；3. 通过系统研究现有指标，识别与阈值区间选择相关的评估偏差，采用跨完整阈值谱聚合检测质量的方法，消除评估不一致性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据上的大量实验表明，该指标提供了稳定、有区分度和可解释的评估，与十种广泛使用的指标相比实现了稳健的评估。

Conclusion: 该研究从检测语义的角度重新审视时间序列异常检测评估，提出的新指标能够更全面、可靠地评估检测性能，解决了现有评估实践中的关键问题，为领域提供了更客观的评估框架。
