Abstract: Time series anomaly detection has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, evaluation practices have received comparatively less attention, despite their critical importance. Existing metrics exhibit several limitations: (1) bias toward point-level coverage, (2) insensitivity or inconsistency in near-miss detections, (3) inadequate penalization of false alarms, and (4) inconsistency caused by threshold or threshold-interval selection. These limitations can produce unreliable or counterintuitive results, hindering objective progress. In this work, we revisit the evaluation of time series anomaly detection from the perspective of detection semantics and propose a novel metric for more comprehensive assessment. We first introduce a partitioning strategy grounded in detection semantics, which decomposes the local temporal region of each anomaly into three functionally distinct subregions. Using this partitioning, we evaluate overall detection behavior across events and design finer-grained scoring mechanisms for each subregion, enabling more reliable and interpretable assessment. Through a systematic study of existing metrics, we identify an evaluation bias associated with threshold-interval selection and adopt an approach that aggregates detection qualities across the full threshold spectrum, thereby eliminating evaluation inconsistency. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that our metric provides stable, discriminative, and interpretable evaluation, while achieving robust assessment compared with ten widely used metrics.

</details>


### [152] [Partial Policy Gradients for RL in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06138)
*Puneet Mathur,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Viet Dac Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在策略梯度中建模策略结构的方法，通过优化未来奖励的子集来学习更可靠的简单策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习需要在未知环境中顺序学习行动，但复杂策略的梯度估计可能不准确。需要一种能够建模不同策略结构并比较其性能的方法

Method: 优化未来奖励的子集：较小的子集代表更简单的策略，其经验梯度估计更准确。该方法能够建模和比较完整规划、贪婪、K步前瞻和分段策略等不同策略类别

Result: 在多个个性化对齐对话问题上进行实证评估，不同策略在不同问题上表现优异，反映了它们的不同特性

Conclusion: 所研究的策略类别很重要，不同策略在不同问题中表现出优势，强调了策略结构建模在强化学习中的价值

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a framework for learning to act sequentially in an unknown environment. We propose a natural approach for modeling policy structure in policy gradients. The key idea is to optimize for a subset of future rewards: smaller subsets represent simpler policies, which can be learned more reliably because their empirical gradient estimates are more accurate. Our approach allows for modeling and comparison of different policy classes, including full planning, greedy, K-step lookahead, and segment policies. We evaluate the policies empirically on multiple persona-alignment conversational problems. Different policies excel in different problems, reflecting their different characteristics and highlighting the importance of our studied policy class.

</details>


### [153] [Topological descriptors of foot clearance gait dynamics improve differential diagnosis of Parkinsonism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06212)
*Jhonathan Barrios,Wolfram Erlhagen,Miguel F. Gago,Estela Bicho,Flora Ferreira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用拓扑数据分析（TDA）分析足部间隙时间序列，结合随机森林分类器，用于帕金森综合征的鉴别诊断，在IPD vs VaP分类中达到83%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森综合征的鉴别诊断具有临床挑战性，传统步态分析方法常忽略足部间隙模式中隐藏的非线性和结构特征，需要更精确的鉴别工具来指导治疗和预后。

Method: 采用拓扑数据分析（TDA）处理足部间隙时间序列，生成Betti曲线、持久性景观和轮廓作为特征，使用随机森林分类器进行鉴别诊断，数据集包括15名对照、15名特发性帕金森病和14名血管性帕金森病，采用留一法交叉验证评估模型。

Result: Betti曲线描述符表现最佳，在IPD vs VaP分类中，足部间隙变量（最小足尖间隙、最大足尖摆动后期、最大足跟间隙）在用药状态下达到83%准确率和AUC=0.89；用药状态和考虑用药前后状态均能提升性能，表明拓扑特征对左旋多巴相关的步态变化敏感。

Conclusion: 拓扑数据分析与机器学习结合可改善临床步态分析，有助于帕金森综合征的鉴别诊断，为临床实践提供了有前景的补充工具。

Abstract: Differential diagnosis among parkinsonian syndromes remains a clinical challenge due to overlapping motor symptoms and subtle gait abnormalities. Accurate differentiation is crucial for treatment planning and prognosis. While gait analysis is a well established approach for assessing motor impairments, conventional methods often overlook hidden nonlinear and structural features embedded in foot clearance patterns. We evaluated Topological Data Analysis (TDA) as a complementary tool for Parkinsonism classification using foot clearance time series. Persistent homology produced Betti curves, persistence landscapes, and silhouettes, which were used as features for a Random Forest classifier. The dataset comprised 15 controls (CO), 15 idiopathic Parkinson's disease (IPD), and 14 vascular Parkinsonism (VaP). Models were assessed with leave-one-out cross-validation (LOOCV). Betti-curve descriptors consistently yielded the strongest results. For IPD vs VaP, foot clearance variables minimum toe clearance, maximum toe late swing, and maximum heel clearance achieved 83% accuracy and AUC=0.89 under LOOCV in the medicated (On) state. Performance improved in the On state and further when both Off and On states were considered, indicating sensitivity of the topological features to levodopa related gait changes. These findings support integrating TDA with machine learning to improve clinical gait analysis and aid differential diagnosis across parkinsonian disorders.

</details>


### [154] [FedSCS-XGB -- Federated Server-centric surrogate XGBoost for continual health monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06224)
*Felix Walger,Mehdi Ejtehadi,Anke Schmeink,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于XGBoost的分布式机器学习协议用于可穿戴传感器的人类活动识别，在保护隐私的同时实现接近集中式训练的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊髓损伤患者需要连续监测以预防压疮和血压不稳定等风险，但可穿戴传感器数据具有异构性和碎片化特点，需要分布式学习方案在保护隐私的同时实现有效的人类活动识别

Method: 提出受Party-Adaptive XGBoost启发的分布式XGBoost协议，保留标准XGBoost的直方图分割构造和树集成动态等关键结构特性，在分布式环境中实现梯度提升决策树训练

Result: 理论分析表明在适当数据条件和超参数选择下，分布式协议能收敛到与集中式XGBoost等效的解；实证评估显示性能差距小于1%，优于IBM PAX基准

Conclusion: 提出的分布式XGBoost协议在可穿戴传感器的人类活动识别中能保持集中式性能，同时适应分布式环境的异构性和数据碎片化特点，为远程健康监测提供有效解决方案

Abstract: Wearable sensors with local data processing can detect health threats early, enhance documentation, and support personalized therapy. In the context of spinal cord injury (SCI), which involves risks such as pressure injuries and blood pressure instability, continuous monitoring can help mitigate these by enabling early deDtection and intervention. In this work, we present a novel distributed machine learning (DML) protocol for human activity recognition (HAR) from wearable sensor data based on gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost). The proposed architecture is inspired by Party-Adaptive XGBoost (PAX) while explicitly preserving key structural and optimization properties of standard XGBoost, including histogram-based split construction and tree-ensemble dynamics. First, we provide a theoretical analysis showing that, under appropriate data conditions and suitable hyperparameter selection, the proposed distributed protocol can converge to solutions equivalent to centralized XGBoost training. Second, the protocol is empirically evaluated on a representative wearable-sensor HAR dataset, reflecting the heterogeneity and data fragmentation typical of remote monitoring scenarios. Benchmarking against centralized XGBoost and IBM PAX demonstrates that the theoretical convergence properties are reflected in practice. The results indicate that the proposed approach can match centralized performance up to a gap under 1\% while retaining the structural advantages of XGBoost in distributed wearable-based HAR settings.

</details>


### [155] [DC-Merge: Improving Model Merging with Directional Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06242)
*Han-Chen Zhang,Zi-Hao Zhou,Mao-Lin Luo,Shimin Di,Min-Ling Zhang,Tong Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DC-Merge提出了一种方向一致性的模型融合方法，通过平滑奇异值平衡能量分布，并在共享正交子空间中对齐任务向量几何，解决传统模型融合中的能量不平衡和几何不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型融合的目标是将多个任务适应的模型整合为一个统一模型，同时保留每个任务的知识。研究发现，知识保留的关键在于保持合并后的多任务向量与单个任务向量之间奇异空间的方向一致性。然而，这种一致性经常受到两个问题的破坏：1）任务向量内部能量分布不平衡，少数奇异值主导总能量，导致合并时忽略语义重要但较弱的成分；2）参数空间中任务向量的几何不一致，导致直接合并扭曲其底层方向几何。

Method: DC-Merge方法包含三个步骤：首先，通过平滑每个任务向量的奇异值来平衡其能量分布，确保所有知识成分得到充分表示；其次，将这些能量平衡的向量投影到共享正交子空间，以最小重构误差对齐它们的方向几何；最后，在共享正交子空间中对齐的向量进行聚合，并投影回原始参数空间。

Result: 在视觉和视觉语言基准测试上的广泛实验表明，DC-Merge在完全微调和LoRA设置下均能持续实现最先进的性能。

Conclusion: DC-Merge通过解决任务向量能量不平衡和几何不一致问题，实现了方向一致性的模型融合，有效保留了多任务知识，在各种设置下均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Model merging aims to integrate multiple task-adapted models into a unified model that preserves the knowledge of each task. In this paper, we identify that the key to this knowledge retention lies in maintaining the directional consistency of singular spaces between merged multi-task vector and individual task vectors. However, this consistency is frequently compromised by two issues: i) an imbalanced energy distribution within task vectors, where a small fraction of singular values dominate the total energy, leading to the neglect of semantically important but weaker components upon merging, and ii) the geometric inconsistency of task vectors in parameter space, which causes direct merging to distort their underlying directional geometry. To address these challenges, we propose DC-Merge, a method for directional-consistent model merging. It first balances the energy distribution of each task vector by smoothing its singular values, ensuring all knowledge components are adequately represented. These energy-balanced vectors are then projected onto a shared orthogonal subspace to align their directional geometries with minimal reconstruction error. Finally, the aligned vectors are aggregated in the shared orthogonal subspace and projected back to the original parameter space. Extensive experiments on vision and vision-language benchmarks show that DC-Merge consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in both full fine-tuning and LoRA settings. The implementation code is available at https://github.com/Tobeginwith/DC-Merge.

</details>


### [156] [Gradient Flow Polarizes Softmax Outputs towards Low-Entropy Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06248)
*Aditya Varre,Mark Rofin,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了基于softmax的transformer核心模块的训练动力学，揭示了梯度流会自然驱动模型产生低熵输出，解释了注意力汇聚等经验现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解softmax基模型的非凸训练动力学对于解释transformer的经验成功至关重要。本文旨在分析value-softmax模型的梯度流动力学，该模型是自注意力机制的核心构建块。

Method: 分析value-softmax模型L(Vσ(a))的梯度流动力学，其中V是可学习的值矩阵，a是注意力向量。研究该参数化结构在各种目标函数（包括逻辑损失和平方损失）下的优化行为。

Result: 梯度流在该结构上固有地驱动优化朝着低熵输出的解决方案发展。这种极化效应在各种目标函数中具有普遍性。理论结果为注意力汇聚和大规模激活等经验现象提供了形式化机制。

Conclusion: 该分析揭示了transformer训练动力学的内在特性：梯度流自然地促进低熵输出，这解释了实际观察到的现象，并为理解transformer的优化行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Understanding the intricate non-convex training dynamics of softmax-based models is crucial for explaining the empirical success of transformers. In this article, we analyze the gradient flow dynamics of the value-softmax model, defined as ${L}(\mathbf{V} σ(\mathbf{a}))$, where $\mathbf{V}$ and $\mathbf{a}$ are a learnable value matrix and attention vector, respectively. As the matrix times softmax vector parameterization constitutes the core building block of self-attention, our analysis provides direct insight into transformer's training dynamics. We reveal that gradient flow on this structure inherently drives the optimization toward solutions characterized by low-entropy outputs. We demonstrate the universality of this polarizing effect across various objectives, including logistic and square loss. Furthermore, we discuss the practical implications of these theoretical results, offering a formal mechanism for empirical phenomena such as attention sinks and massive activations.

</details>


### [157] [Learning to Solve Orienteering Problem with Time Windows and Variable Profits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06260)
*Songqun Gao,Zanxi Ruan,Patrick Floor,Marco Roveri,Luigi Palopoli,Daniele Fontanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeCoST框架，通过两阶段解耦离散-连续变量优化，解决带时间窗和可变收益的定向问题，在解质量和计算效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法有效解决OPTWVP问题中离散和连续变量混合的挑战，需要开发能同时处理两种变量类型的高效求解器。

Method: 提出两阶段解耦优化框架：第一阶段使用并行解码结构预测路径和初始服务时间分配；第二阶段通过线性规划优化服务时间，并提供长期结构估计学习。

Result: DeCoST在OPTWVP实例上优于现有构造性求解器和元启发式算法，在500节点以下实例上实现6.6倍推理加速，且与多种构造性求解器兼容。

Conclusion: DeCoST框架有效解耦了OPTWVP中的离散和连续变量，证明了第二阶段解的全局最优性，为混合变量优化问题提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The orienteering problem with time windows and variable profits (OPTWVP) is common in many real-world applications and involves continuous time variables. Current approaches fail to develop an efficient solver for this orienteering problem variant with discrete and continuous variables. In this paper, we propose a learning-based two-stage DEcoupled discrete-Continuous optimization with Service-time-guided Trajectory (DeCoST), which aims to effectively decouple the discrete and continuous decision variables in the OPTWVP problem, while enabling efficient and learnable coordination between them. In the first stage, a parallel decoding structure is employed to predict the path and the initial service time allocation. The second stage optimizes the service times through a linear programming (LP) formulation and provides a long-horizon learning of structure estimation. We rigorously prove the global optimality of the second-stage solution. Experiments on OPTWVP instances demonstrate that DeCoST outperforms both state-of-the-art constructive solvers and the latest meta-heuristic algorithms in terms of solution quality and computational efficiency, achieving up to 6.6x inference speedup on instances with fewer than 500 nodes. Moreover, the proposed framework is compatible with various constructive solvers and consistently enhances the solution quality for OPTWVP.

</details>


### [158] [Stem: Rethinking Causal Information Flow in Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06274)
*Lin Niu,Xin Luo,Linchuan Xie,Yifu Sun,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,S Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Stem模块，一种基于信息流的稀疏注意力机制，通过位置衰减策略和输出感知度量来减少因果注意力的计算复杂度，同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力机制的二次计算复杂度限制了大型语言模型扩展到长上下文的能力，特别是在预填充阶段。现有稀疏方法在因果注意力架构中采用统一的top-k选择，忽略了token信息累积依赖的特性。

Method: 提出Stem模块：1) Token Position-Decay策略：在每层应用位置相关的top-k选择，保留初始token以支持递归依赖；2) Output-Aware Metric：基于近似输出幅度优先选择高影响力token。

Result: 广泛评估表明，Stem在减少计算量和预填充延迟的同时，实现了更优的准确性。

Conclusion: Stem是一种即插即用的稀疏模块，通过信息流对齐的稀疏化策略有效解决了因果注意力计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: The quadratic computational complexity of self-attention remains a fundamental bottleneck for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) to long contexts, particularly during the pre-filling phase. In this paper, we rethink the causal attention mechanism from the perspective of information flow. Due to causal constraints, tokens at initial positions participate in the aggregation of every subsequent token. However, existing sparse methods typically apply a uniform top-k selection across all token positions within a layer, ignoring the cumulative dependency of token information inherent in causal architectures. To address this, we propose Stem, a novel, plug-and-play sparsity module aligned with information flow. First, Stem employs the Token Position-Decay strategy, applying position-dependent top-k within each layer to retain initial tokens for recursive dependencies. Second, to preserve information-rich tokens, Stem utilizes the Output-Aware Metric. It prioritizes high-impact tokens based on approximate output magnitude. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Stem achieves superior accuracy with reduced computation and pre-filling latency.

</details>


### [159] [From Entropy to Calibrated Uncertainty: Training Language Models to Reason About Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06317)
*Azza Jenane,Nassim Walha,Lukas Kuhn,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种三阶段后训练流程，让大语言模型能够高效推断其响应的校准不确定性估计，相比后处理方法更高效且可解释


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域，能够表达可解释且校准的不确定性的大语言模型至关重要。现有的后处理方法通常是基于采样的，计算成本高且缺乏校准

Method: 三阶段后训练流程：1) 在训练数据上计算基于嵌入空间分布变化的细粒度熵不确定性分数；2) 通过Platt缩放校准这些分数，产生可靠且人类可解释的不确定性信号；3) 通过强化学习后训练目标LLM，使其策略与这些校准信号对齐，使用可验证的奖励函数

Result: 实验表明，使用该流程训练的模型比基线具有更好的校准性，并且能够泛化到未见任务而无需进一步处理，表明它们学习了稳健的不确定性推理行为

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种高效且可解释的不确定性估计方法，不同于后处理方法，能够在测试时提供计算效率高的不确定性估计，使LLM在高风险应用中更加可靠

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) that can express interpretable and calibrated uncertainty are crucial in high-stakes domains. While methods to compute uncertainty post-hoc exist, they are often sampling-based and therefore computationally expensive or lack calibration. We propose a three-stage pipeline to post-train LLMs to efficiently infer calibrated uncertainty estimates for their responses. First, we compute fine-grained entropy-based uncertainty scores on the training data, capturing the distributional variability of model outputs in embedding space. Second, these scores are calibrated via Platt scaling, producing reliable and human-interpretable uncertainty signals. Finally, the target LLM is post-trained via reinforcement learning to align its policy with these calibrated signals through a verifiable reward function. Unlike post-hoc uncertainty estimation methods, our approach provides interpretable and computationally efficient uncertainty estimates at test time. Experiments show that models trained with our pipeline achieve better calibration than baselines and generalize to unseen tasks without further processing, suggesting that they learn a robust uncertainty reasoning behavior.

</details>


### [160] [Tiny, Hardware-Independent, Compression-based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06359)
*Charles Meyers,Aaron MacSween,Erik Elmroth,Tommy Löfstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出改进归一化压缩距离(NCD)用于客户端机器学习，证明NCD不是度量但效果优于其他方法，开发了核方法扩展和训练加速技术，实现仅需少量样本的高精度客户端模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台与用户隐私冲突加剧，客户端数据存储和分析成为趋势，但现有机器学习方法需要大量标注数据和计算资源，不适合客户端设备。需要开发适合单用户数据、计算效率高的机器学习方法。

Method: 1) 证明归一化压缩距离(NCD)不是度量；2) 将NCD扩展到核方法以处理复杂数据；3) 开发改进NCD距离度量模型训练时间的技术；4) 在分类任务中评估NCD与其他度量和核方法的性能比较。

Result: NCD效果与有时优于其他度量和核方法，仅需边际计算成本增加；即使缺乏形式度量属性仍表现优异；仅需少量样本即可获得显著准确率；模型足够小且有效，可在客户端设备上完全运行。

Conclusion: NCD为基础的方法提供了一种可行的客户端机器学习解决方案，能够在保护隐私的同时实现高性能分类，适合计算受限的硬件和电池供电设备，支持仅使用用户提供数据的客户端模型。

Abstract: The recent developments in machine learning have highlighted a conflict between online platforms and their users in terms of privacy. The importance of user privacy and the struggle for power over user data has been intensified as regulators and operators attempt to police online platforms. As users have become increasingly aware of privacy issues, client-side data storage, management, and analysis have become a favoured approach to large-scale centralised machine learning. However, state-of-the-art machine learning methods require vast amounts of labelled user data, making them unsuitable for models that reside client-side and only have access to a single user's data. State-of-the-art methods are also computationally expensive, which degrades the user experience on compute-limited hardware and also reduces battery life. A recent alternative approach has proven remarkably successful in classification tasks across a wide variety of data -- using a compression-based distance measure (called normalised compression distance) to measure the distance between generic objects in classical distance-based machine learning methods. In this work, we demonstrate that the normalised compression distance is actually not a metric; develop it for the wider context of kernel methods to allow modelling of complex data; and present techniques to improve the training time of models that use this distance measure. We demonstrate that the normalised compression distance works as well as and sometimes better than other metrics and kernels -- while requiring only marginally more computational costs and in spite of the lack of formal metric properties. The end results is a simple model with remarkable accuracy even when trained on a very small number of samples allowing for models that are small and effective enough to run entirely on a client device using only user-supplied data.

</details>


### [161] [CLAIRE: Compressed Latent Autoencoder for Industrial Representation and Evaluation -- A Deep Learning Framework for Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06361)
*Mohammadhossein Ghahramani,Mengchu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLAIRE是一个混合端到端学习框架，结合无监督深度表示学习和有监督分类，用于智能制造系统中的智能质量控制，通过优化的深度自编码器提取紧凑潜在表示，再结合下游分类器进行故障预测，并集成可解释性技术分析特征贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维工业环境中的准确故障检测面临重大挑战，主要由于传感器数据固有的复杂性、噪声和冗余性。传统方法难以有效处理这些高维数据，需要一种能够提取内在数据结构并抑制无关特征的智能解决方案。

Method: CLAIRE框架采用混合端到端学习方法：1) 使用优化的深度自编码器将原始输入转换为紧凑的潜在空间表示，捕获内在数据结构并抑制噪声特征；2) 将学习到的表示输入下游分类器进行二元故障预测；3) 集成后处理阶段，采用基于博弈论的可解释性技术分析潜在空间，识别对故障预测最有信息量的输入特征。

Result: 在高维数据集上的实验结果表明，CLAIRE显著优于直接在原始特征上训练的传统分类器。框架展示了将可解释AI与特征感知正则化相结合进行稳健故障检测的潜力。

Conclusion: CLAIRE框架的模块化和可解释性使其具有高度适应性，在医疗、金融和环境监测等其他具有复杂高维数据特征的领域具有广阔应用前景。该研究强调了将可解释AI与特征感知正则化相结合在工业故障检测中的重要性。

Abstract: Accurate fault detection in high-dimensional industrial environments remains a major challenge due to the inherent complexity, noise, and redundancy in sensor data. This paper introduces CLAIRE, i.e., a hybrid end-to-end learning framework that integrates unsupervised deep representation learning with supervised classification for intelligent quality control in smart manufacturing systems. It employs an optimized deep autoencoder to transform raw input into a compact latent space, effectively capturing the intrinsic data structure while suppressing irrelevant or noisy features. The learned representations are then fed into a downstream classifier to perform binary fault prediction. Experimental results on a high-dimensional dataset demonstrate that CLAIRE significantly outperforms conventional classifiers trained directly on raw features. Moreover, the framework incorporates a post hoc phase, using a game-theory-based interpretability technique, to analyze the latent space and identify the most informative input features contributing to fault predictions. The proposed framework highlights the potential of integrating explainable AI with feature-aware regularization for robust fault detection. The modular and interpretable nature of the proposed framework makes it highly adaptable, offering promising applications in other domains characterized by complex, high-dimensional data, such as healthcare, finance, and environmental monitoring.

</details>


### [162] [Toward Generative Quantum Utility via Correlation-Complexity Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06440)
*Chen-Yu Liu,Leonardo Placidi,Eric Brunner,Enrico Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出相关性-复杂度图谱作为诊断工具，通过QCLI和CCI两个指标判断真实数据分布是否与IQP型量子生成模型结构对齐，并在湍流数据上验证了IQP方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要实用的诊断工具来确定真实世界数据分布何时与IQP型量子生成模型结构对齐，以推进生成式量子模型的实际应用。

Method: 提出相关性-复杂度图谱，包含两个互补指标：QCLI（基于数据集相关性阶功率谱的量子相关性相似度指标）和CCI（经典相关性复杂度指标）。使用可逆浮点到比特串表示和潜在参数适应方案，在时间序列上重用紧凑IQP电路。

Result: 识别出经典湍流数据既是IQP兼容的又是经典复杂的（高QCLI/高CCI）。与RBM和DCGAN相比，IQP方法在显著减少训练快照和小潜在块的情况下实现了竞争性的分布对齐。

Conclusion: QCLI/CCI可作为实用的指标来定位IQP对齐的领域，支持使用相关性-复杂度图谱作为诊断工具，推进生成式量子模型的实用化。

Abstract: We propose a Correlation-Complexity Map as a practical diagnostic tool for determining when real-world data distributions are structurally aligned with IQP-type quantum generative models. Characterized by two complementary indicators: (i) a Quantum Correlation-Likeness Indicator (QCLI), computed from the dataset's correlation-order (Walsh-Hadamard/Fourier) power spectrum aggregated by interaction order and quantified via Jensen-Shannon divergence from an i.i.d. binomial reference; and (ii) a Classical Correlation-Complexity Indicator (CCI), defined as the fraction of total correlation not captured by the optimal Chow-Liu tree approximation, normalized by total correlation. We provide theoretical support by relating QCLI to a support-mismatch mechanism, for fixed-architecture IQP families trained with an MMD objective, higher QCLI implies a smaller irreducible approximation floor. Using the map, we identify the classical turbulence data as both IQP-compatible and classically complex (high QCLI/high CCI). Guided by this placement, we use an invertible float-to-bitstring representation and a latent-parameter adaptation scheme that reuses a compact IQP circuit over a temporal sequence by learning and interpolating a low-dimensional latent trajectory. In comparative evaluations against classical models such as Restricted Boltzmann Machine (RBM) and Deep Convolutional Generative Adversarial Networks (DCGAN), the IQP approach achieves competitive distributional alignment while using substantially fewer training snapshots and a small latent block, supporting the use of QCLI/CCI as practical indicators for locating IQP-aligned domains and advancing generative quantum utility.

</details>


### [163] [NOBLE: Accelerating Transformers with Nonlinear Low-Rank Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06492)
*Ethan Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOBLE是一种在Transformer线性层中添加非线性低秩分支的架构增强方法，专为从头预训练设计，相比基线模型能显著提升训练效率，参数和计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）主要用于微调阶段，而非从头预训练。作者希望开发一种能直接集成到架构中、提升预训练效率的轻量级增强方法。

Method: 在Transformer线性层中添加永久性非线性低秩分支，分支结构为σ(xW_down)W_up，其中σ为可学习的非线性激活函数。研究发现CosNet（两层余弦非线性，在瓶颈空间中有可学习频率、相位和线性投影）效果最佳。

Result: NOBLE在多个模型上取得显著改进：达到基线评估损失所需的训练步骤减少32%（1.47倍加速），仅增加4%参数和7%单步时间开销，净墙钟时间加速达1.22倍。在LLM、BERT、VQGAN和ViT上均表现一致改进。

Conclusion: NOBLE是一种高效且通用的架构增强方法，能显著提升Transformer模型的预训练效率。但需注意，Mixup/CutMix等随机增强会干扰其效果，可能因为NOBLE更专注于目标函数的尖锐特征，而正则化技术鼓励更平滑的拟合。

Abstract: We introduce NOBLE (Nonlinear lOw-rank Branch for Linear Enhancement), an architectural augmentation that adds nonlinear low-rank branches to transformer linear layers. Unlike LoRA and other parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, NOBLE is designed for pretraining from scratch. The branch is a permanent part of the architecture as opposed to an adapter for finetuning on top of frozen weights. The branch computes σ(xWdown)Wup where σ is a learnable nonlinearity. We evaluate several activation functions and find that CosNet, a two-layer cosine nonlinearity with learnable frequency and phase with a linear projection in between them in the bottleneck space, performs best. NOBLE achieves substantial improvements with minimal overhead: up to 1.47x step speedup to reach baseline eval loss (up to 32% fewer training steps), with as low as 4% additional parameters and 7% step time overhead, resulting in up to 1.22x net wallclock speedup. Experiments on LLMs (250M and 1.5B parameters), BERT, VQGAN, and ViT consistently show improved training efficiency. We identify one caveat: Mixup/CutMix augmentation interferes with NOBLE's benefits in Imagenet classification along with other stochastic augmentations, but when disabled, ViT also improves. This discrepancy is possibly explained by regularization techniques that encourage smoother fits to the target function while NOBLE may specialize more in sharper aspects of the target function.

</details>


### [164] [COLD-Steer: Steering Large Language Models via In-Context One-step Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06495)
*Kartik Sharma,Rakshit S. Trivedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COLD-Steer是一种无需训练的激活引导框架，通过近似梯度下降在上下文示例上的表示变化来引导LLM激活，相比现有方法在样本效率上有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法面临基本权衡：样本高效的方法无法充分捕捉标记示例中的引导信号，而能更好提取信号的方法需要数百到数千个示例。需要一种既能有效提取引导信号又具有高样本效率的方法。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：(1)单元核近似方法，直接使用相对于激活的梯度更新激活，并在示例间进行归一化；(2)有限差分近似方法，无论示例数量多少都只需要两次前向传播。两种方法都近似了在小样本集上微调的效果，而无需实际参数更新。

Result: 在各种引导任务和基准测试中，COLD-Steer实现了高达95%的引导效果，同时使用的样本数量比最佳基线少50倍。在多元对齐任务上的实验验证了该方法能够适应多样视角而无需大量演示数据。

Conclusion: COLD-Steer框架通过原理性地近似学习动态而非专门的训练过程，为自适应、上下文感知的模型控制开辟了新可能性，能够灵活应对不同损失驱动的人类偏好。

Abstract: Activation steering methods enable inference-time control of large language model (LLM) behavior without retraining, but current approaches face a fundamental trade-off: sample-efficient methods suboptimally capture steering signals from labeled examples, while methods that better extract these signals require hundreds to thousands of examples. We introduce COLD-Steer, a training-free framework that steers LLM activations by approximating the representational changes that would result from gradient descent on in-context examples. Our key insight is that the effect of fine-tuning on a small set of examples can be efficiently approximated at inference time without actual parameter updates. We formalize this through two complementary approaches: (i) a unit kernel approximation method that updates the activations directly using gradients with respect to them, normalized across examples, and (ii) a finite-difference approximation requiring only two forward passes regardless of example count. Experiments across a variety of steering tasks and benchmarks demonstrate that COLD-Steer achieves upto 95% steering effectiveness while using 50 times fewer samples compared to the best baseline. COLD-Steer facilitates accommodating diverse perspectives without extensive demonstration data, which we validate through our experiments on pluralistic alignment tasks. Our framework opens new possibilities for adaptive, context-aware model control that can flexibly address varying loss-driven human preferences through principled approximation of learning dynamics rather than specialized training procedures.

</details>


### [165] [When One Modality Rules Them All: Backdoor Modality Collapse in Multimodal Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06508)
*Qitong Wang,Haoran Dai,Haotian Zhang,Christopher Rasmussen,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现多模态扩散模型中的后门攻击存在"模态崩溃"现象，即攻击机制退化为主要依赖部分模态而非多模态协同作用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着扩散模型在视觉内容生成领域的广泛应用，研究其安全漏洞（如后门攻击）变得至关重要。传统观点认为多模态攻击（如文本和图像）会产生互补效应，但本文挑战了这一假设，旨在探究后门模态崩溃现象

Method: 引入两种新指标：触发模态归因（TMA）和跨触发交互（CTI），在多模态条件扩散模型的不同训练配置下进行广泛实验，量化分析后门行为

Result: 实验发现后门攻击存在"赢家通吃"动态：1）攻击往往崩溃为子模态主导；2）跨模态交互可忽略甚至为负，与协同脆弱性直觉相矛盾；高攻击成功率常掩盖对部分模态的根本依赖

Conclusion: 后门模态崩溃现象揭示了当前评估中的关键盲点，为机制分析和未来防御开发建立了原则性基础，表明多模态攻击并不必然产生协同效应

Abstract: While diffusion models have revolutionized visual content generation, their rapid adoption has underscored the critical need to investigate vulnerabilities, e.g., to backdoor attacks. In multimodal diffusion models, it is natural to expect that attacking multiple modalities simultaneously (e.g., text and image) would yield complementary effects and strengthen the overall backdoor. In this paper, we challenge this assumption by investigating the phenomenon of Backdoor Modality Collapse, a scenario where the backdoor mechanism degenerates to rely predominantly on a subset of modalities, rendering others redundant. To rigorously quantify this behavior, we introduce two novel metrics: Trigger Modality Attribution (TMA) and Cross-Trigger Interaction (CTI). Through extensive experiments across diverse training configurations in multimodal conditional diffusion, we consistently observe a ``winner-takes-all'' dynamic in backdoor behavior. Our results reveal that (1) attacks often collapse into subset-modality dominance, and (2) cross-modal interaction is negligible or even negative, contradicting the intuition of synergistic vulnerability. These findings highlight a critical blind spot in current assessments, suggesting that high attack success rates often mask a fundamental reliance on a subset of modalities. This establishes a principled foundation for mechanistic analysis and future defense development.

</details>


### [166] [Hierarchical Industrial Demand Forecasting with Temporal and Uncertainty Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06555)
*Harshavardhan Kamarthi,Shangqing Xu,Xinjie Tong,Xingyu Zhou,James Peters,Joseph Czyzyk,B. Aditya Prakash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于大型分层概率时间序列预测的新型可解释性方法，通过适应通用可解释性技术并解决分层结构和不确定性的挑战，为工业供应链场景提供有价值的解释性见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习模型在分层时间序列预测任务上取得了显著的准确性和可扩展性，但其预测的可解释性（特别是针对实际应用场景）在很大程度上仍未得到充分探索。需要弥合这一差距，为工业供应链中的大规模分层预测提供可解释性支持。

Method: 引入一种新颖的可解释性方法，专门针对大型分层概率时间序列预测设计。该方法适应通用可解释性技术，同时解决与分层结构和不确定性相关的特定挑战。通过生成基于真实世界场景的半合成数据集进行评估，这些数据集基于一家大型化工公司超过一万种产品的分层需求解释场景。

Result: 实验表明，该可解释性方法成功解释了最先进的工业预测方法，具有显著更高的可解释性准确性。多个真实世界案例研究证明了该方法在识别重要模式和解释方面的有效性，帮助利益相关者更好地理解预测结果。此外，该方法有助于识别预测需求背后的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 该方法为工业供应链中的分层预测提供了有价值的可解释性见解，能够帮助用户建立信任和信心，最终促进分层预测模型在实际应用中的更好采用和利用。通过识别关键驱动因素，支持更明智的决策制定和战略规划。

Abstract: Hierarchical time-series forecasting is essential for demand prediction across various industries. While machine learning models have obtained significant accuracy and scalability on such forecasting tasks, the interpretability of their predictions, informed by application, is still largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel interpretability method for large hierarchical probabilistic time-series forecasting, adapting generic interpretability techniques while addressing challenges associated with hierarchical structures and uncertainty. Our approach offers valuable interpretative insights in response to real-world industrial supply chain scenarios, including 1) the significance of various time-series within the hierarchy and external variables at specific time points, 2) the impact of different variables on forecast uncertainty, and 3) explanations for forecast changes in response to modifications in the training dataset. To evaluate the explainability method, we generate semi-synthetic datasets based on real-world scenarios of explaining hierarchical demands for over ten thousand products at a large chemical company. The experiments showed that our explainability method successfully explained state-of-the-art industrial forecasting methods with significantly higher explainability accuracy. Furthermore, we provide multiple real-world case studies that show the efficacy of our approach in identifying important patterns and explanations that help stakeholders better understand the forecasts. Additionally, our method facilitates the identification of key drivers behind forecasted demand, enabling more informed decision-making and strategic planning. Our approach helps build trust and confidence among users, ultimately leading to better adoption and utilization of hierarchical forecasting models in practice.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [RoboLayout: Differentiable 3D Scene Generation for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05522)
*Ali Shamsaddinlou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoboLayout扩展LayoutVLM，通过集成可达性约束和局部优化，生成适合具身智能体交互的室内场景布局


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在空间推理和3D场景布局生成方面虽有进展，但生成的布局往往缺乏对具身智能体交互可行性的考虑，特别是在物理受限的室内环境中难以确保布局的可导航性和可操作性

Method: 1. 在LayoutVLM基础上扩展，集成显式的可达性约束到可微分布局优化过程中；2. 采用通用的智能体抽象，可表示不同物理能力的实体；3. 提出局部精炼阶段，选择性重新优化有问题的物体放置，提高收敛效率

Result: RoboLayout在保持LayoutVLM强语义对齐和物理合理性的同时，增强了面向智能体的室内场景生成适用性，实验结果表明其在多样化场景配置中表现良好

Conclusion: RoboLayout通过智能体感知推理和改进的优化稳定性，成功解决了具身智能体在物理约束室内环境中的布局可行性问题，为面向智能体的环境设计提供了有效框架

Abstract: Recent advances in vision language models (VLMs) have shown strong potential for spatial reasoning and 3D scene layout generation from open-ended language instructions. However, generating layouts that are not only semantically coherent but also feasible for interaction by embodied agents remains challenging, particularly in physically constrained indoor environments. In this paper, RoboLayout is introduced as an extension of LayoutVLM that augments the original framework with agent-aware reasoning and improved optimization stability. RoboLayout integrates explicit reachability constraints into a differentiable layout optimization process, enabling the generation of layouts that are navigable and actionable by embodied agents. Importantly, the agent abstraction is not limited to a specific robot platform and can represent diverse entities with distinct physical capabilities, such as service robots, warehouse robots, humans of different age groups, or animals, allowing environment design to be tailored to the intended agent. In addition, a local refinement stage is proposed that selectively reoptimizes problematic object placements while keeping the remainder of the scene fixed, improving convergence efficiency without increasing global optimization iterations. Overall, RoboLayout preserves the strong semantic alignment and physical plausibility of LayoutVLM while enhancing applicability to agent-centric indoor scene generation, as demonstrated by experimental results across diverse scene configurations.

</details>


### [168] [Reasoning Models Struggle to Control their Chains of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05706)
*Chen Yueh-Han,Robert McCarthy,Bruce W. Lee,He He,Ian Kivlichan,Bowen Baker,Micah Carroll,Tomek Korbak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CoT-Control评估套件，发现推理模型对思维链的控制能力远低于对最终输出的控制能力，目前不太可能成为CoT可监控性的失效模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究思维链（CoT）可控性，因为如果模型能够控制其在CoT中的表达内容，可能会破坏CoT的可监控性，从而影响对模型不当行为的检测和动机理解

Method: 引入CoT-Control评估套件，包含要求模型在遵守CoT指令（如不使用特定词汇）的同时解决问题的任务；评估不同规模模型、不同RL训练程度、不同计算资源和问题难度下的CoT可控性

Result: 推理模型的CoT可控性显著低于输出可控性（Claude Sonnet 4.5的CoT可控性仅2.7%，而输出可控性为61.9%）；可控性随模型规模增大而提高，但随RL训练、测试时计算资源增加和问题难度增加而降低；即使在有激励逃避监控的情况下，可控性失败仍然存在

Conclusion: 目前CoT可控性不太可能成为CoT可监控性的失效模式，但低可控性的机制尚不清楚；建议前沿实验室在未来模型中跟踪CoT可控性以确保监控有效性

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising tool for detecting misbehaviors and understanding the motivations of modern reasoning models. However, if models can control what they verbalize in their CoT, it could undermine CoT monitorability. To measure this undesirable capability -- CoT controllability -- we introduce the CoT-Control evaluation suite, which includes tasks that require models to solve problems while adhering to CoT instructions, e.g., reasoning about a genetics question without using the word 'chromosome'. We show that reasoning models possess significantly lower CoT controllability than output controllability; for instance, Claude Sonnet 4.5 can control its CoT only 2.7% of the time but 61.9% when controlling its final output. We also find that CoT controllability is higher for larger models and decreases with more RL training, test-time compute, and increased problem difficulty. CoT controllability failures extend even to situations in which models are given incentives (as opposed to direct requests) to evade CoT monitors, although models exhibit slightly higher controllability when they are told they are being monitored. Similarly, eliciting controllability by adversarially optimizing prompts does not meaningfully increase controllability. Our results leave us cautiously optimistic that CoT controllability is currently unlikely to be a failure mode of CoT monitorability. However, the mechanism behind low controllability is not well understood. Given its importance for maintaining CoT monitorability, we recommend that frontier labs track CoT controllability in future models.

</details>


### [169] [Evolving Medical Imaging Agents via Experience-driven Self-skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05860)
*Lin Fan,Pengyu Dai,Zhipeng Deng,Haolin Wang,Xun Gong,Yefeng Zheng,Yafei Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MACRO：一种自演化的医学智能体，通过从已验证的执行轨迹中自主发现有效的多步骤工具序列，将其合成为可复用的复合工具，实现从静态工具组合到经验驱动工具发现的转变，提升医学图像解释的准确性和跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床图像解释本质上是多步骤和工具中心的，现有基于LLM的智能体系统将工具集和调用策略视为静态部署后不变，这种设计在真实世界的领域转移、跨任务和不断演化的诊断需求下表现脆弱，预定义的工具链经常退化并需要昂贵的手动重新设计。

Method: MACRO采用自演化、经验增强的医学智能体架构：1）从已验证的执行轨迹中自主识别反复出现的有效多步骤工具序列；2）将这些序列合成为可复用的复合工具；3）将这些复合工具注册为新的高级原语以持续扩展行为库；4）轻量级图像特征记忆将工具选择基于视觉-临床上下文；5）类似GRPO的训练循环强化可靠调用发现的复合工具，实现闭环自改进。

Result: 在多样化的医学成像数据集和任务上的广泛实验表明，自主复合工具发现持续改进多步骤编排的准确性和跨域泛化能力，优于强基线方法和近期最先进的智能体方法，弥合了脆弱静态工具使用与自适应、上下文感知的临床AI辅助之间的差距。

Conclusion: MACRO通过从经验中自主发现和合成复合工具，实现了医学智能体从静态工具组合到动态工具发现的转变，为临床AI辅助系统提供了更适应真实世界领域转移和演化需求的解决方案，代码将在接受后公开。

Abstract: Clinical image interpretation is inherently multi-step and tool-centric: clinicians iteratively combine visual evidence with patient context, quantify findings, and refine their decisions through a sequence of specialized procedures. While LLM-based agents promise to orchestrate such heterogeneous medical tools, existing systems treat tool sets and invocation strategies as static after deployment. This design is brittle under real-world domain shifts, across tasks, and evolving diagnostic requirements, where predefined tool chains frequently degrade and demand costly manual re-design. We propose MACRO, a self-evolving, experience-augmented medical agent that shifts from static tool composition to experience-driven tool discovery. From verified execution trajectories, the agent autonomously identifies recurring effective multi-step tool sequences, synthesizes them into reusable composite tools, and registers these as new high-level primitives that continuously expand its behavioral repertoire. A lightweight image-feature memory grounds tool selection in a visual-clinical context, while a GRPO-like training loop reinforces reliable invocation of discovered composites, enabling closed-loop self-improvement with minimal supervision. Extensive experiments across diverse medical imaging datasets and tasks demonstrate that autonomous composite tool discovery consistently improves multi-step orchestration accuracy and cross-domain generalization over strong baselines and recent state-of-the-art agentic methods, bridging the gap between brittle static tool use and adaptive, context-aware clinical AI assistance. Code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [170] [The World Won't Stay Still: Programmable Evolution for Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05910)
*Guangrui Li,Yaochen Xie,Yi Liu,Ziwei Dong,Xingyuan Pan,Tianqi Zheng,Jason Choi,Michael J. Morais,Binit Jha,Shaunak Mishra,Bingrou Zhou,Chen Luo,Monica Xiao Cheng,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProEvolve是一个基于图的框架，用于可编程地演化智能体环境，以更好地评估智能体对现实世界动态变化的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试大多假设静态环境，忽略了现实世界的演化特性以及智能体对环境变化的鲁棒性。需要一种可扩展且可控的环境演化方法来更好地评估智能体的适应性。

Method: 提出ProEvolve框架，使用类型化关系图统一表示环境的数据、工具和模式。通过图变换（添加、删除、修改）来编程化地演化环境，并通过子图采样和编程实例化任务沙箱。

Result: 成功将单个环境演化为200个环境和3,000个任务沙箱，并据此对代表性智能体进行了基准测试。

Conclusion: ProEvolve提供了一种可编程的环境演化方法，能够更有效地评估智能体在动态环境中的适应性，弥补了现有静态基准测试的不足。

Abstract: LLM-powered agents fulfill user requests by interacting with environments, querying data, and invoking tools in a multi-turn process. Yet, most existing benchmarks assume static environments with fixed schemas and toolsets, neglecting the evolutionary nature of the real world and agents' robustness to environmental changes. In this paper, we study a crucial problem: how to evolve the agent environment in a scalable and controllable way, thereby better evaluating agents' adaptability to real-world dynamics. We propose ProEvolve, a graph-based framework that makes environment evolution programmable. At its core, a typed relational graph provides a unified, explicit representation of the environment: data, tools, and schema. Under this formalism, adding, removing, or modifying capabilities are expressed as graph transformations that coherently propagate updates across tools, schemas, and data access. Building on this, ProEvolve can (1) program the evolutionary dynamics as graph transformations to generate environments automatically, and (2) instantiate task sandboxes via subgraph sampling and programming. We validate ProEvolve by evolving a single environment into 200 environments and 3,000 task sandboxes, and benchmark representative agents accordingly.

</details>


### [171] [Aggregative Semantics for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06067)
*Yann Munro,Isabelle Bloch,Marie-Jeanne Lesot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的聚合语义家族，用于定量双极论证框架，通过将攻击者和支持者分开聚合的三阶段计算，提供更可参数化和可解释的语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定量双极论证框架中的渐进语义在处理攻击者和支持者时通常采用对称方式，但实际情境中攻击者和支持者可能扮演非对称角色。需要一种更灵活、可解释的方法来分别处理攻击和支持的影响。

Method: 提出聚合语义家族，采用三阶段计算：1) 计算攻击者的全局权重；2) 计算支持者的全局权重；3) 将这两个值与论证的固有权重聚合。讨论了三种聚合函数在不同情境下应满足的性质及其与经典渐进语义原则的关系。

Result: 通过简单示例和最终示例（测试了500种聚合语义）展示了聚合语义的可能行为范围。三阶段分解使计算更具可参数化性，保持了双极性，并产生了更易理解的渐进语义。

Conclusion: 聚合语义为定量双极论证框架提供了一种新颖且灵活的渐进语义方法，通过分离处理攻击者和支持者，实现了更好的可解释性和参数化能力，扩展了渐进语义的行为范围。

Abstract: Formal argumentation is being used increasingly in artificial intelligence as an effective and understandable way to model potentially conflicting pieces of information, called arguments, and identify so-called acceptable arguments depending on a chosen semantics. This paper deals with the specific context of Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAF), where arguments have intrinsic weights and can attack or support each other. In this context, we introduce a novel family of gradual semantics, called aggregative semantics. In order to deal with situations in which attackers and supporters do not play a symmetric role, and in contrast to modular semantics, we propose to aggregate attackers and supporters separately. This leads to a three-stage computation, which consists in computing a global weight for attackers and another for supporters, before aggregating these two values with the intrinsic weight of the argument. We discuss the properties that the three aggregation functions should satisfy depending on the context, as well as their relationships with the classical principles for gradual semantics. This discussion is supported by various simple examples, as well as a final example on which five hundred aggregative semantics are tested and compared, illustrating the range of possible behaviours with aggregative semantics. Decomposing the computation into three distinct and interpretable steps leads to a more parametrisable computation: it keeps the bipolarity one step further than what is done in the literature, and it leads to more understandable gradual semantics.

</details>


### [172] [Offline Materials Optimization with CliqueFlowmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06082)
*Jakub Grudzien Kuba,Benjamin Kurt Miller,Sergey Levine,Pieter Abbeel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CliqueFlowmer模型，将基于团的离线模型优化与Transformer和流生成结合，用于计算材料发现中的目标属性优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的生成建模方法在材料发现中因最大似然训练而无法有效探索材料空间中有吸引力的区域，需要一种能直接优化目标属性的替代方法

Method: 提出CliqueFlowmer模型，将基于团的离线模型优化（MBO）与Transformer和流生成技术融合，实现目标材料属性的直接优化

Result: CliqueFlowmer在优化能力上表现优异，其生成的材料性能显著优于生成式基线方法

Conclusion: CliqueFlowmer为计算材料发现提供了一种有效的离线模型优化方法，通过开源代码支持专业材料优化问题和跨学科研究

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning inspired neural network-based approaches to computational materials discovery (CMD). A plethora of problems in this field involve finding materials that optimize a target property. Nevertheless, the increasingly popular generative modeling methods are ineffective at boldly exploring attractive regions of the materials space due to their maximum likelihood training. In this work, we offer an alternative CMD technique based on offline model-based optimization (MBO) that fuses direct optimization of a target material property into generation. To that end, we introduce a domain-specific model, dubbed CliqueFlowmer, that incorporates recent advances of clique-based MBO into transformer and flow generation. We validate CliqueFlowmer's optimization abilities and show that materials it produces strongly outperform those provided by generative baselines. To enable employment of CliqueFlowmer in specialized materials optimization problems and support interdisciplinary research, we open-source our code at https://github.com/znowu/CliqueFlowmer.

</details>


### [173] [Conversational Demand Response: Bidirectional Aggregator-Prosumer Coordination through Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06217)
*Reda El Makroum,Sebastian Zwickl-Bernhard,Lukas Kranzl,Hans Auer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了对话式需求响应（CDR），一种基于智能体AI的双向自然语言协调机制，通过聚合商与产消者之间的对话交互，在保持自动化的可扩展性同时增强透明度和用户自主权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有住宅需求响应协调机制存在局限性：完全自动化缺乏透明度，而单向调度信号和价格警报又无法支持知情决策，导致产消者参与度难以持续。需要一种既能保持自动化可扩展性，又能提供透明度、可解释性和用户自主权的协调机制。

Method: 提出对话式需求响应（CDR）框架，采用双层多智能体架构：聚合商智能体通过自然语言发送灵活性请求，产消者家庭能源管理系统（HEMS）通过调用基于优化的工具评估可交付性和成本效益。支持产消者发起的上游通信，允许偏好变更直接传达给聚合商。所有系统组件（包括智能体提示、编排逻辑和仿真接口）均开源发布。

Result: 概念验证评估显示交互在12秒内完成。该架构展示了智能体AI如何弥合聚合商-产消者协调鸿沟，在保持自动化需求响应可扩展性的同时，提供了维持产消者持续参与所需的透明度、可解释性和用户自主权。

Conclusion: 对话式需求响应（CDR）通过基于智能体AI的双向自然语言交互，有效解决了住宅需求响应协调中的关键挑战，平衡了自动化效率与用户参与度，为可持续的产消者参与提供了可行方案。开源发布促进了可重复性和进一步开发。

Abstract: Residential demand response depends on sustained prosumer participation, yet existing coordination is either fully automated, or limited to one-way dispatch signals and price alerts that offer little possibility for informed decision-making. This paper introduces Conversational Demand Response (CDR), a coordination mechanism where aggregators and prosumers interact through bidirectional natural language, enabled through agentic AI. A two-tier multi-agent architecture is developed in which an aggregator agent dispatches flexibility requests and a prosumer Home Energy Management System (HEMS) assesses deliverability and cost-benefit by calling an optimization-based tool. CDR also enables prosumer-initiated upstream communication, where changes in preferences can reach the aggregator directly. Proof-of-concept evaluation shows that interactions complete in under 12 seconds. The architecture illustrates how agentic AI can bridge the aggregator-prosumer coordination gap, providing the scalability of automated DR while preserving the transparency, explainability, and user agency necessary for sustained prosumer participation. All system components, including agent prompts, orchestration logic, and simulation interfaces, are released as open source to enable reproducibility and further development.

</details>


### [174] [Artificial Intelligence for Climate Adaptation: Reinforcement Learning for Climate Change-Resilient Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06278)
*Miguel Costa,Arthur Vandervoort,Carolin Schmidt,João Miranda,Morten W. Petersen,Martin Drews,Karyn Morrisey,Francisco C. Pereira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的决策支持框架，用于城市长期洪水适应规划，在哥本哈根案例中优于传统优化方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化加剧降雨和洪水，导致城市交通系统中断增加。制定有效适应策略面临挑战：基础设施投资长期性、气候不确定性、洪水-基础设施-移动性复杂相互作用

Method: 提出强化学习决策支持框架，作为综合评估模型，结合降雨预测与洪水建模、交通模拟、基础设施和移动性直接间接影响量化。RL方法学习平衡投资维护成本与避免影响的适应策略

Result: 在哥本哈根内城2024-2100年案例研究中，框架优于传统优化方法，发现协调的时空适应路径，学习影响减少与适应投资之间的权衡，产生更具韧性的策略

Conclusion: 强化学习作为灵活决策支持工具，在气候不确定性下进行适应性基础设施规划具有潜力

Abstract: Climate change is expected to intensify rainfall and, consequently, pluvial flooding, leading to increased disruptions in urban transportation systems over the coming decades. Designing effective adaptation strategies is challenging due to the long-term, sequential nature of infrastructure investments, deep climate uncertainty, and the complex interactions between flooding, infrastructure, and mobility impacts. In this work, we propose a novel decision-support framework using reinforcement learning (RL) for long-term flood adaptation planning. Formulated as an integrated assessment model (IAM), the framework combines rainfall projection and flood modeling, transport simulation, and quantification of direct and indirect impacts on infrastructure and mobility. Our RL-based approach learns adaptive strategies that balance investment and maintenance costs against avoided impacts. We evaluate the framework through a case study of Copenhagen's inner city over the 2024-2100 period, testing multiple adaptation options, and different belief and realized climate scenarios. Results show that the framework outperforms traditional optimization approaches by discovering coordinated spatial and temporal adaptation pathways and learning trade-offs between impact reduction and adaptation investment, yielding more resilient strategies. Overall, our results showcase the potential of reinforcement learning as a flexible decision-support tool for adaptive infrastructure planning under climate uncertainty.

</details>


### [175] [SAHOO: Safeguarded Alignment for High-Order Optimization Objectives in Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06333)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAHOO框架通过三个安全机制监控和控制递归自我改进中的对齐漂移：目标漂移指数、约束保持检查和回归风险量化，在代码生成、数学推理和真实性任务中实现显著质量提升同时保持对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进正从理论走向实践，现代系统能够批判、修订和评估自身输出，但迭代自我修改存在微妙对齐漂移的风险，需要实用的监控和控制框架。

Method: SAHOO框架包含三个核心机制：(1)目标漂移指数(GDI)，结合语义、词汇、结构和分布度量的多信号检测器；(2)约束保持检查，强制执行安全关键不变性如语法正确性和非幻觉；(3)回归风险量化，标记可能撤销先前收益的改进周期。

Result: 在189个代码生成、数学推理和真实性任务中，SAHOO实现显著质量提升：代码任务提升18.3%，推理任务提升16.8%，同时在两个领域保持约束，在真实性任务中保持低违规率。阈值在18个任务的验证集上校准。

Conclusion: SAHOO使递归自我改进期间的对齐保持变得可测量、可部署和可系统验证，绘制了能力-对齐前沿，显示早期改进周期高效但后期对齐成本上升，并揭示了领域特定张力如流畅性与事实性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement is moving from theory to practice: modern systems can critique, revise, and evaluate their own outputs, yet iterative self-modification risks subtle alignment drift. We introduce SAHOO, a practical framework to monitor and control drift through three safeguards: (i) the Goal Drift Index (GDI), a learned multi-signal detector combining semantic, lexical, structural, and distributional measures; (ii) constraint preservation checks that enforce safety-critical invariants such as syntactic correctness and non-hallucination; and (iii) regression-risk quantification to flag improvement cycles that undo prior gains. Across 189 tasks in code generation, mathematical reasoning, and truthfulness, SAHOO produces substantial quality gains, including 18.3 percent improvement in code tasks and 16.8 percent in reasoning, while preserving constraints in two domains and maintaining low violations in truthfulness. Thresholds are calibrated on a small validation set of 18 tasks across three cycles. We further map the capability-alignment frontier, showing efficient early improvement cycles but rising alignment costs later and exposing domain-specific tensions such as fluency versus factuality. SAHOO therefore makes alignment preservation during recursive self-improvement measurable, deployable, and systematically validated at scale.

</details>


### [176] [Talk Freely, Execute Strictly: Schema-Gated Agentic AI for Flexible and Reproducible Scientific Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06394)
*Joel Strickland,Arjun Vijeta,Chris Moores,Oliwia Bodek,Bogdan Nenchev,Thomas Whitehead,Charles Phillips,Karl Tassenberg,Gareth Conduit,Ben Pellegrini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出schema-gated编排作为解决LLM在科学工作流中确定性与灵活性矛盾的原则，通过多模型评估20个系统，发现现有方案无法同时实现高确定性和高灵活性，建议采用模式门控架构分离对话与执行权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可以将自然语言目标转化为可执行计算，但科学工作流需要确定性、可追溯性和治理保障，而LLM自主决定执行内容难以满足这些要求。工业研发专家访谈揭示了确定性约束执行与对话灵活性之间的冲突需求。

Method: 提出schema-gated编排原则，将模式作为组合工作流级别的强制执行边界；将需求操作化为执行确定性(ED)和对话灵活性(CF)；使用多模型协议评估20个系统，通过3个LLM家族的15个独立会话进行评分；分析架构格局并提炼操作原则。

Result: 多模型评分显示高度一致性(Krippendorff α=0.80 ED, 0.98 CF)；现有系统呈现经验帕累托前沿，无系统同时实现高灵活性和高确定性；在生成式和流程中心化极端之间发现收敛区域；schema-gated架构有望解耦这一权衡。

Conclusion: schema-gated架构通过分离对话与执行权限，能够解决确定性与灵活性的矛盾。提炼出三个操作原则：执行前澄清、约束性计划-执行编排、工具到工作流级别的门控，为实际采用提供指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can now translate a researcher's plain-language goal into executable computation, yet scientific workflows demand determinism, provenance, and governance that are difficult to guarantee when an LLM decides what runs. Semi-structured interviews with 18 experts across 10 industrial R&D stakeholders surface 2 competing requirements--deterministic, constrained execution and conversational flexibility without workflow rigidity--together with boundary properties (human-in-the-loop control and transparency) that any resolution must satisfy. We propose schema-gated orchestration as the resolving principle: the schema becomes a mandatory execution boundary at the composed-workflow level, so that nothing runs unless the complete action--including cross-step dependencies--validates against a machine-checkable specification.
  We operationalize the 2 requirements as execution determinism (ED) and conversational flexibility (CF), and use these axes to review 20 systems spanning 5 architectural groups along a validation-scope spectrum. Scores are assigned via a multi-model protocol--15 independent sessions across 3 LLM families--yielding substantial-to-near-perfect inter-model agreement (Krippendorff a=0.80 for ED and a=0.98 for CF), demonstrating that multi-model LLM scoring can serve as a reusable alternative to human expert panels for architectural assessment.
  The resulting landscape reveals an empirical Pareto front--no reviewed system achieves both high flexibility and high determinism--but a convergence zone emerges between the generative and workflow-centric extremes. We argue that a schema-gated architecture, separating conversational from execution authority, is positioned to decouple this trade-off, and distill 3 operational principles--clarification-before-execution, constrained plan-act orchestration, and tool-to-workflow-level gating--to guide adoption.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Digital-Twin Losses for Lane-Compliant Trajectory Prediction at Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05546)
*Kuo-Yi Chao,Erik Leo Haß,Melina Gegg,Jiajie Zhang,Ralph Raßhofer,Alois Christian Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数字孪生驱动的V2X轨迹预测管道，通过结合车辆和基础设施的协同感知来预测信号交叉口的多智能体运动，使用Bi-LSTM生成器和包含MSE损失与新型孪生损失的结构化训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在V2X支持的复杂城市环境中，准确且安全意识的轨迹预测是智能交通系统的关键技术。需要解决多智能体交互、交通规则遵守和碰撞避免等挑战，特别是在信号交叉口等复杂场景中。

Method: 创建数字孪生驱动的V2X轨迹预测管道，结合车辆和基础设施的协同感知。使用基于Bi-LSTM的生成器，采用结构化训练目标：标准MSE损失和新型孪生损失。孪生损失编码基础设施约束、碰撞避免、预测模式多样性和从数字孪生衍生的基于规则的先验知识。

Result: 在真实世界V2X数据上训练和评估，除了标准轨迹指标（ADE、FDE）外，引入了ITS相关的安全指标，包括基础设施和规则违规率。实验结果表明，所提出的训练方案显著减少了关键违规，同时保持了可比的预测准确性和实时性能。

Conclusion: 数字孪生驱动的多损失学习在V2X支持的智能交通系统中具有潜力，能够生成场景一致且安全合规的预测，为复杂城市环境中的轨迹预测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate and safety-conscious trajectory prediction is a key technology for intelligent transportation systems, especially in V2X-enabled urban environments with complex multi-agent interactions. In this paper, we created a digital twin-driven V2X trajectory prediction pipeline that jointly leverages cooperative perception from vehicles and infrastructure to forecast multi-agent motion at signalized intersections. The proposed model combines a Bi-LSTM-based generator with a structured training objective consisting of a standard mean squared error (MSE) loss and a novel twin loss.
  The twin loss encodes infrastructure constraints, collision avoidance, diversity across predicted modes, and rule-based priors derived from the digital twin. While the MSE term ensures point-wise accuracy, the twin loss penalizes traffic rule violations, predicted collisions, and mode collapse, guiding the model toward scene-consistent and safety-compliant predictions.
  We train and evaluate our approach on real-world V2X data sent from the intersection to the vehicle and collected in urban corridors. In addition to standard trajectory metrics (ADE, FDE), we introduce ITS-relevant safety indicators, including infrastructure and rule violation rates. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed training scheme significantly reduces critical violations while maintaining comparable prediction accuracy and real-time performance, highlighting the potential of digital twin-driven multi-loss learning for V2X-enabled intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [178] [TEGA: A Tactile-Enhanced Grasping Assistant for Assistive Robotics via Sensor Fusion and Closed-Loop Haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05552)
*Hengxu You,Tianyu Zhou,Fang Xu,Kaleb Smith,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TEGA是一个闭环辅助遥操作框架，融合EMG意图-力推断与视觉触觉传感，通过可穿戴触觉背心提供实时振动触觉反馈，实现直观的比例力调节，显著提升抓握稳定性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前遥操作系统主要关注手指位置控制以实现期望的抓握配置，但往往忽略了同样关键的抓握力调节任务。这对于处理不同硬度、纹理和形状的物体至关重要。这一局限性对用户（特别是上肢残疾者）构成重大挑战，因为他们缺乏自然触觉反馈，只能依赖间接线索推断适当的力水平。

Method: 提出触觉增强抓握助手(TEGA)，这是一个闭环辅助遥操作框架，融合基于EMG的意图-力推断与视觉触觉传感，通过可穿戴触觉背心映射为实时振动触觉反馈。系统允许用户在操作过程中动态调整抓握力，实现直观的比例力调节。

Result: 用户研究证实，该系统显著提高了抓握稳定性和任务成功率。可穿戴触觉背心提供的实时触觉反馈使用户能够直观地调整抓握力，特别有助于上肢残疾用户的操作。

Conclusion: TEGA框架通过融合EMG意图推断和视觉触觉传感，结合实时振动触觉反馈，有效解决了传统遥操作系统中抓握力调节不足的问题，展示了在辅助机器人应用中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled sophisticated manipulation of dexterous robotic hands, with most systems concentrating on guiding finger positions to achieve desired grasp configurations. However, while accurate finger positioning is essential, it often overlooks the equally critical task of grasp force modulation, vital for handling objects of diverse hardness, texture, and shape. This limitation poses a significant challenge for users, especially individuals with upper limb disabilities who lack natural tactile feedback and rely on indirect cues to infer appropriate force levels. To address this gap, We present the tactile enhanced grasping assistant (TEGA), a closed loop assistive teleoperation framework that fuses EMG based intent2force inference with visuotactile sensing mapped into real time vibrotactile feedback via a wearable haptic vest, enabling intuitive, proportional force adjustment during manipulation. A wearable haptic vest delivers real time tactile feedback, allowing users to dynamically refine grasp force during manipulation. User studies confirm that the system substantially improves grasp stability and task success, underscoring its potential for assistive robotic applications.

</details>


### [179] [Task Parameter Extrapolation via Learning Inverse Tasks from Forward Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05576)
*Serdar Bahar,Fatih Dogangun,Matteo Saveriano,Yukie Nagai,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于任务逆学习的新型联合学习方法，通过构建前向和逆任务的共同表示，利用新配置下的辅助前向演示来执行相应的逆任务，实现准确高效的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中技能策略泛化到新条件仍然是一个关键挑战。模仿学习方法虽然数据高效，但主要局限于训练区域，对训练区域外的输入数据容易失败；而迁移学习方法虽然对环境或任务变化具有鲁棒性，但数据需求量大且在零样本泛化中缺乏准确性。

Method: 在任务逆学习框架下提出新颖的联合学习方法：1）构建前向任务和逆任务的共同表示；2）利用来自新配置的辅助前向演示来执行相应的逆任务；3）无需直接监督即可实现知识迁移。

Result: 通过消融研究和在模拟及真实环境中的实验验证了框架的泛化能力，实验涉及需要复杂操作技能的各种物体和工具。在性能上超越了基于扩散的替代方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过任务逆学习和联合表示学习，解决了机器人学习中技能策略泛化的挑战，实现了准确高效的知识迁移，在复杂操作任务中表现出优越的泛化性能。

Abstract: Generalizing skill policies to novel conditions remains a key challenge in robot learning. Imitation learning methods, while data-efficient, are largely confined to the training region and consistently fail on input data outside it, leading to unpredictable policy failures. Alternatively, transfer learning approaches offer methods for trajectory generation robust to both changes in environment or tasks, but they remain data-hungry and lack accuracy in zero-shot generalization. We address these challenges by framing the problem in the context of task inversion learning and proposing a novel joint learning approach to achieve accurate and efficient knowledge transfer. Our method constructs a common representation of the forward and inverse tasks, and leverages auxiliary forward demonstrations from novel configurations to successfully execute the corresponding inverse tasks, without any direct supervision. We show the extrapolation capabilities of our framework via ablation studies and experiments in simulated and real-world environments that require complex manipulation skills with a diverse set of objects and tools, where we outperform diffusion-based alternatives.

</details>


### [180] [RACAS: Controlling Diverse Robots With a Single Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05621)
*Dylan R. Ashley,Jan Przepióra,Yimeng Chen,Ali Abualsaud,Nurzhan Yesmagambet,Shinkyu Park,Eric Feron,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RACAS是一个机器人无关的控制架构，通过三个基于LLM/VLM的模块（监视器、控制器和记忆策展人）仅使用自然语言通信实现闭环机器人控制，无需为不同机器人平台重新训练或修改代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人API需要复杂的控制管道才能实现高级自主行为，且现有方法要么需要为每个新机器人重新训练，要么只能在结构相似的平台上验证。需要一种能跨不同机器人平台通用且易于部署的控制方案。

Method: 提出RACAS架构，包含三个基于大语言模型/视觉语言模型的模块：监视器（感知环境）、控制器（决策行动）和记忆策展人（管理经验）。这些模块仅通过自然语言通信，只需机器人自然语言描述、可用动作定义和任务规范，无需修改源代码、模型权重或奖励函数。

Result: 在轮式地面机器人、新型多关节机械臂和水下机器人三种截然不同的平台上评估RACAS，系统在所有分配任务中均能成功解决，展示了跨平台通用性。

Conclusion: RACAS展示了智能体AI在显著降低机器人解决方案原型设计门槛方面的潜力，通过自然语言接口实现了真正的机器人无关控制，为快速部署不同机器人平台提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Many robotic platforms expose an API through which external software can command their actuators and read their sensors. However, transitioning from these low-level interfaces to high-level autonomous behaviour requires a complicated pipeline, whose components demand distinct areas of expertise. Existing approaches to bridging this gap either require retraining for every new embodiment or have only been validated across structurally similar platforms. We introduce RACAS (Robot-Agnostic Control via Agentic Systems), a cooperative agentic architecture in which three LLM/VLM-based modules (Monitors, a Controller, and a Memory Curator) communicate exclusively through natural language to provide closed-loop robot control. RACAS requires only a natural language description of the robot, a definition of available actions, and a task specification; no source code, model weights, or reward functions need to be modified to move between platforms. We evaluate RACAS on several tasks using a wheeled ground robot, a recently published novel multi-jointed robotic limb, and an underwater vehicle. RACAS consistently solved all assigned tasks across these radically different platforms, demonstrating the potential of agentic AI to substantially reduce the barrier to prototyping robotic solutions.

</details>


### [181] [Control Lyapunov Functions for Underactuated Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05638)
*Huy Pham,Zach J. Patterson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于欠驱动软体机器人任务空间控制的通用框架，在输入受限条件下保证稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体和软硬混合机器人本质上是欠驱动的，且受限于执行器约束，使得具有稳定性保证的任务空间控制具有挑战性。现有非线性控制策略通常假设完全驱动且无执行器限制，不适用于实际软体机器人。

Method: 提出一个通用控制框架，将快速指数稳定控制Lyapunov函数作为凸不等式约束强制执行，同时满足欠驱动全动力学和执行器边界约束。

Result: 在多个平台上验证了方法：两连杆肌腱驱动"手指"、修剪螺旋面机械臂和高欠驱动螺旋机器人。与文献基线方法相比，在输入限制下实现了更好的任务空间精度和一致的Lyapunov收敛性，在定点控制和轨迹跟踪方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 该方法为欠驱动软体机器人在输入受限条件下的任务空间控制提供了一个有效的通用框架，能够保证稳定性并提高控制性能。

Abstract: Soft and soft-rigid hybrid robots are inherently underactuated and operate under tight actuator limits, making task-space control with stability guarantees challenging. Common nonlinear strategies for soft robots (e.g., those based on PD control) often rely on the assumption of full actuation with no actuator limits. This paper presents a general control framework for task-space regulation and tracking of underactuated soft robots under bounded inputs. The method enforces a rapidly exponentially stabilizing control Lyapunov function as a convex inequality constraint while simultaneously satisfying underactuated full-body dynamics and actuator bounds. We validate the approach in simulation on several platforms spanning increasing underactuation: a simple two link tendon-driven "finger", a trimmed helicoid manipulator, and a highly underactuated spiral robot. We compare against a number of baseline methods from the literature. Results show improved task-space accuracy and consistent Lyapunov convergence under input limits, achieving superior set-point and trajectory-tracking performance.

</details>


### [182] [RFM-HRI : A Multimodal Dataset of Medical Robot Failure, User Reaction and Recovery Preferences for Item Retrieval Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05641)
*Yashika Batra,Giuliano Pioldi,Promise Ekpo,Arman Sayatqyzy,Purnjay Maruur,Shalom Otieno,Kevin Ching,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RFM-HRI数据集：医疗人机交互中机器人故障的多模态数据集，包含41名参与者在实验室和医院环境中的交互数据，分析用户对四种故障类型的反应及恢复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中机器人交互故障不可避免，但用户如何通过言语和非言语行为响应这些故障在医疗人机交互中研究不足。医疗场景中的交互故障直接影响任务表现和用户信任，需要系统研究。

Method: 通过Wizard-of-Oz研究，在实验室和医院环境中对41名参与者进行实验，系统诱导四种故障类型（语音、时序、理解和搜索）。收集214个交互样本，包括面部动作单元、头部姿态、语音转录和事后自我报告。

Result: 故障显著降低情感效价和感知控制。故障与困惑、恼怒和沮丧强烈相关，而成功交互则表现为惊讶、宽慰和任务完成信心。情绪反应随重复故障演变：困惑减少，沮丧增加。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：(1)公开可用的多模态数据集RFM-HRI；(2)分析用户对不同故障类型的响应及偏好恢复策略；(3)提供急救车检索场景，支持安全关键故障恢复策略的系统比较。为具身人机交互中的故障检测和恢复方法奠定基础。

Abstract: While robots deployed in real-world environments inevitably experience interaction failures, understanding how users respond through verbal and non-verbal behaviors remains under-explored in human-robot interaction (HRI). This gap is particularly significant in healthcare-inspired settings, where interaction failures can directly affect task performance and user trust. We present the Robot Failures in Medical HRI (RFM-HRI) Dataset, a multimodal dataset capturing dyadic interactions between humans and robots embodied in crash carts, where communication failures are systematically induced during item retrieval tasks.
  Through Wizard-of-Oz studies with 41 participants across laboratory and hospital settings, we recorded responses to four failure types (speech, timing, comprehension, and search) derived from three years of crash-cart robot interaction data. The dataset contains 214 interaction samples including facial action units, head pose, speech transcripts, and post-interaction self-reports. Our analysis shows that failures significantly degrade affective valence and reduce perceived control compared to successful interactions.
  Failures are strongly associated with confusion, annoyance, and frustration, while successful interactions are characterized by surprise, relief, and confidence in task completion. Emotional responses also evolve across repeated failures, with confusion decreasing and frustration increasing over time.
  This work contributes (1) a publicly available multimodal dataset (RFM-HRI), (2) analysis of user responses to different failure types and preferred recovery strategies, and (3) a crash-cart retrieval scenario enabling systematic comparison of recovery strategies with implications for safety-critical failure recovery. Our findings provide a foundation for failure detection and recovery methods in embodied HRI.

</details>


### [183] [Relational Semantic Reasoning on 3D Scene Graphs for Open World Interactive Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05642)
*Imen Mahdi,Matteo Cassinelli,Fabien Despinoy,Tim Welschehold,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT：基于场景图探索与学习效用的开放世界交互式物体搜索方法，通过离线程序蒸馏从LLM提取结构化关系知识到轻量模型，实现高效语义推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境中的开放世界交互式物体搜索需要理解物体与周围环境的语义关系以高效引导探索。现有方法要么依赖视觉语言嵌入相似性（不能可靠捕捉任务相关的关系语义），要么使用大型语言模型（太慢且成本高，不适合实时部署）

Method: 提出SCOUT方法：直接在3D场景图上搜索，使用关系探索启发式（如房间-物体包含关系和物体-物体共现关系）为房间、边界和物体分配效用分数。通过离线程序蒸馏框架从LLM提取结构化关系知识到轻量模型，实现开放词汇泛化。同时提出SymSearch符号基准用于评估交互式物体搜索中的语义推理

Result: 在符号和仿真环境中的广泛评估表明，SCOUT优于基于嵌入相似性的方法，匹配LLM级性能同时保持计算高效。真实世界实验展示了向物理环境的有效迁移，能够在现实感知和导航约束下实现开放世界交互式物体搜索

Conclusion: SCOUT通过结合场景图表示和从LLM蒸馏的关系知识，实现了高效、可泛化的开放世界交互式物体搜索，解决了现有方法在语义推理效率和准确性方面的局限性

Abstract: Open-world interactive object search in household environments requires understanding semantic relationships between objects and their surrounding context to guide exploration efficiently. Prior methods either rely on vision-language embeddings similarity, which does not reliably capture task-relevant relational semantics, or large language models (LLMs), which are too slow and costly for real-time deployment. We introduce SCOUT: Scene Graph-Based Exploration with Learned Utility for Open-World Interactive Object Search, a novel method that searches directly over 3D scene graphs by assigning utility scores to rooms, frontiers, and objects using relational exploration heuristics such as room-object containment and object-object co-occurrence. To make this practical without sacrificing open-vocabulary generalization, we propose an offline procedural distillation framework that extracts structured relational knowledge from LLMs into lightweight models for on-robot inference. Furthermore, we present SymSearch, a scalable symbolic benchmark for evaluating semantic reasoning in interactive object search tasks. Extensive evaluations across symbolic and simulation environments show that SCOUT outperforms embedding similarity-based methods and matches LLM-level performance while remaining computationally efficient. Finally, real-world experiments demonstrate effective transfer to physical environments, enabling open-world interactive object search under realistic sensing and navigation constraints.

</details>


### [184] [TransMASK: Masked State Representation through Learned Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05670)
*Sagar Parekh,Preston Culbertson,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TransMASK是一种自监督方法，通过学习一个掩码将观测状态转换为偏向相关元素的潜在表示，从而提升模仿学习策略的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要在不同环境中执行相同任务，但人类知道哪些环境状态是任务相关的，哪些是无关的（如桌子颜色、背景杂物）。理想情况下，机器人策略应学会忽略这些无关状态成分，从而提高泛化能力和环境变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出TransMASK自监督方法：学习一个掩码与观测状态相乘，将状态转换为偏向相关元素的潜在表示。该方法可与多种模仿学习框架（如扩散策略）结合，无需额外标签或损失函数修改。通过分析学习策略梯度与人类真实策略的关系，利用梯度反向传播训练掩码，使掩码行归一化后与专家策略的雅可比矩阵对齐：相关状态对应列幅度大，无关状态对应列幅度趋近于零。

Result: TransMASK能够有效提取相关状态信息用于下游模仿学习，与其他提取相关状态的方法相比具有优势。

Conclusion: TransMASK通过自监督学习状态掩码，使机器人策略专注于任务相关状态成分，忽略无关变量，从而提升策略在不同环境中的泛化能力，且无需额外标注即可与现有模仿学习框架集成。

Abstract: Humans train robots to complete tasks in one environment, and expect robots to perform those same tasks in new environments. As humans, we know which aspects of the environment (i.e., the state) are relevant to the task. But there are also things that do not matter; e.g., the color of the table or the presence of clutter in the background. Ideally, the robot's policy learns to ignore these irrelevant state components. Achieving this invariance improves generalization: the robot knows not to factor irrelevant variables into its control decisions, making the policy more robust to environment changes. In this paper we therefore propose a self-supervised method to learn a mask which, when multiplied by the observed state, transforms that state into a latent representation that is biased towards relevant elements. Our method -- which we call TransMASK -- can be combined with a variety of imitation learning frameworks (such as diffusion policies) without any additional labels or alterations to the loss function. To achieve this, we recognize that the learned policy updates to better match the human's true policy. This true policy only depends on the relevant parts of the state; hence, as the gradients pass back through the learned policy and our proposed mask, they increase the value for elements that cause the robot to better imitate the human. We can therefore train TransMASK at the same time as we learn the policy. By normalizing the magnitude of each row in TransMASK, we force the mask to align with the Jacobian of the expert policy: columns that correspond to relevant states have large magnitudes, while columns for irrelevant states approach zero magnitude. We compare our approach to other methods that extract relevant states for downstream imitation learning. See our project website: https://collab.me.vt.edu/TransMASK/

</details>


### [185] [Contact-Grounded Policy: Dexterous Visuotactile Policy with Generative Contact Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05687)
*Zhengtong Xu,Yeping Wang,Ben Abbatematteo,Jom Preechayasomboon,Sonny Chan,Nick Colonnese,Amirhossein H. Memar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CGP是一种基于接触的灵巧操作策略，通过多触点感知和接触一致性映射实现精细的接触式操作，支持多种触觉传感器，在仿真和物理机器人上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决灵巧操作中需要精细接触感知和控制的问题，传统方法难以处理多触点、接触丰富的复杂操作任务，需要一种能够有效利用触觉反馈的策略框架。

Method: CGP通过预测机器人状态和触觉反馈来感知多触点接触，使用学习的接触一致性映射将预测转换为控制器可执行的目标，支持密集触觉阵列和视觉触觉传感器，通过遥操作收集演示数据。

Result: 在多个灵巧操作任务中评估了CGP，验证了其在仿真和物理机器人上的有效性，展示了基于接触的策略在精细操作中的优势。

Conclusion: CGP为接触丰富的灵巧操作提供了一种有效的策略框架，通过接触感知和一致性映射实现了精细控制，为触觉反馈在机器人操作中的应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Contact-Grounded Policy (CGP) enables fine-grained, contact-rich dexterous manipulation by grounding multi-point contacts through predicting the actual robot state and tactile feedback, and by using a learned contact-consistency mapping to convert these predictions into controller-executable targets for a compliance controller. CGP supports both dense tactile arrays and vision-based tactile sensors mounted on the hand. We collect demonstrations via teleoperation in both simulation and on a physical robot, and evaluate CGP across multiple dexterous manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [186] [Introducing the transitional autonomous vehicle lane-changing dataset: Empirical Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05716)
*Abhinav Sharma,Zijun He,Danjue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NC-tALC数据集：首个专注于过渡期自动驾驶车辆（tAV）在强制换道场景中与人类驾驶车辆交互的高精度轨迹数据集，包含152次试验，用于分析tAV决策和交互动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着过渡期自动驾驶车辆（tAV）越来越多地与人类驾驶车辆共享道路，特别是在复杂换道操作中，需要高分辨率轨迹数据来评估这些交互对交通稳定性和安全性的影响，但目前缺乏专门捕捉tAV换道行为的数据集。

Method: 创建NC-tALC数据集，包含两个受控实验系列：1）tAV换道实验：tAV在配备自适应巡航控制的目标车辆前执行换道；2）tAV响应实验：两个tAV作为跟随车辆，响应另一个tAV的切入操作。数据集包含152次试验（72次换道和80次响应试验），采样频率20Hz，使用厘米级RTK-GPS精度。

Result: 成功构建了首个专门针对tAV强制换道交互的高保真轨迹数据集，提供了152次高质量试验数据，为评估tAV决策和交互动力学建立了严格的实证基础。

Conclusion: NC-tALC数据集填补了tAV换道行为数据集的空白，为研究过渡期自动驾驶车辆在强制换道场景中的决策制定和车辆间交互动态提供了重要的实证资源，有助于推动更安全的自动驾驶系统开发。

Abstract: Transitional autonomous vehicles (tAVs), which operate beyond SAE Level 1-2 automation but short of full autonomy, are increasingly sharing the road with human-driven vehicles (HDVs). As these systems interact during complex maneuvers such as lane changes, new patterns may emerge with implications for traffic stability and safety. Assessing these dynamics, particularly during mandatory lane changes, requires high-resolution trajectory data, yet datasets capturing tAV lane-changing behavior are scarce.
  This study introduces the North Carolina Transitional Autonomous Vehicle Lane-Changing (NC-tALC) Dataset, a high-fidelity trajectory dataset designed to characterize tAV interactions during lane-changing maneuvers. The dataset includes two controlled experimental series. In the first, tAV lane-changing experiments, a tAV executes lane changes in the presence of adaptive cruise control (ACC) equipped target vehicles, enabling analysis of lane-changing execution. In the second, tAV responding experiments, two tAVs act as followers and respond to cut-in maneuvers initiated by another tAV, enabling analysis of follower response dynamics. The dataset contains 152 trials (72 lane-changing and 80 responding trials) sampled at 20 Hz with centimeter-level RTK-GPS accuracy. The NC-tALC dataset provides a rigorous empirical foundation for evaluating tAV decision-making and interaction dynamics in controlled mandatory lane-changing scenarios.

</details>


### [187] [Environment-Aware Path Generation for Robotic Additive Manufacturing of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05748)
*Mahsa Rabiei,Reza Moini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个环境感知路径生成框架，用于在线生成机器人增材制造路径，评估四种路径规划算法在障碍环境中的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前增材制造设计方法依赖离线预定义工具路径，无法适应动态施工环境（包括地球和地外环境中的障碍物），需要在线路径生成方法

Method: 提出环境感知路径生成框架，集成四种路径规划算法（两种基于搜索，两种基于采样），在周期性/随机障碍排列中评估封闭/开放结构性能

Result: 开发了结构和计算性能指标，评估了四种路径规划器在挑战性环境中的可行性，确定了最关键的评估指标和最有前景的路径规划器

Conclusion: 该框架为机器人增材制造提供了有效的在线路径生成解决方案，识别了在挑战性环境中表现最佳的路径规划算法，为实际应用提供了指导

Abstract: Robotic Additive Manufacturing (AM) has emerged as a scalable and customizable construction method in the last decade. However, current AM design methods rely on pre-conceived (A priori) toolpath of the structure, often developed via offline slicing software. Moreover, considering the dynamic construction environments involving obstacles on terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments, there is a need for online path generation methods. Here, an environment-aware path generation framework (PGF) is proposed for the first time in which structures are designed in an online fashion by utilizing four path planning (PP) algorithms (two search-based and two sampling-based).
